#2480. « Le jour est venu de rendre le numéro 7 » : Cristiano Ronaldo annonce sa retraite internationale !

Ce jeudi 5 novembre 2026, Cristiano Ronaldo a officialisé sa retraite internationale avec le Portugal à travers une vidéo d'environ cinq minutes et une lettre d'adieu publiées sur ses réseaux sociaux. Âgé de 41 ans, l'attaquant met un terme à une aventure de 23 ans débutée en août 2003 avec la Seleção das Quinas.

Dans sa lettre adressée aux supporters portugais, Cristiano Ronaldo a expliqué que le jour était venu de rendre le maillot numéro 7 et de le transmettre aux générations futures, précisant qu'il s'agissait d'un choix dicté tant par le cœur que par la tête. L'attaquant a également rendu hommage à ses coéquipiers, à ses entraîneurs, à l'ensemble du staff technique et médical, ainsi qu'à sa famille et aux supporters portugais à travers le monde. La fin d'une ère pour la sélection.

Au-delà de ces explications, le joueur a tenu à saluer la ferveur constante du public qui l'a accompagné pendant plus de deux décennies. L'annonce de son départ a d'ailleurs immédiatement suscité l'émotion de son ancien coéquipier Pepe. Ce dernier a ainsi tenu à saluer son parcours sur les réseaux sociaux en commentant la publication : « C'était bien plus que du football. Merci pour tout, Cris ».

Cette annonce vient clore une aventure débutée en août 2003 avec la Seleção das Quinas. En 23 ans de carrière en sélection, Cristiano Ronaldo s'est constitué un bilan sans précédent sur la scène internationale. Il détient en effet le record mondial masculin de sélections et de buts en équipe nationale avec 146 réalisations en 234 matches. Un bilan exceptionnel. Au cours de cette épopée de 23 ans sous le maillot portugais, l'attaquant a marqué l'histoire de son pays.

Avec la sélection portugaise, Cristiano Ronaldo a ainsi remporté l'Euro 2016 ainsi que deux Ligues des nations (2018-2019 et 2024-2025). Toutefois, cette retraite ne concerne que la scène internationale. En effet, le joueur de 41 ans ne met pas un terme à sa carrière sur les terrains et poursuit son parcours en club avec Al-Nassr.

Cette annonce survient dans la foulée de tensions survenues lors du rassemblement de septembre 2026. L'attaquant avait alors quitté la sélection après avoir passé l'intégralité du match sur le banc sans entrer en jeu contre la Norvège sous les ordres de Jorge Jesus. Malgré cet épisode houleux et une sanction de 30 jours purgée, Cristiano Ronaldo avait tout de même reçu une pré-convocation pour le rassemblement de novembre avant de choisir de raccrocher définitivement en sélection.

D'ailleurs, la Fédération portugaise de football a publié un communiqué pour saluer son capitaine emblématique et a annoncé qu'un hommage officiel digne de son immense carrière lui serait prochainement rendu. Désormais, l'équipe nationale doit se projeter sur la suite de la compétition. Le Portugal disputera sa prochaine rencontre officielle le samedi 14 novembre 2026 à 20h45 face au Danemark à l'Estádio Municipal de Braga en Ligue des nations.

#2479. « Un vide impossible à combler » : La famille de Jesús de Miguel sort du silence après le drame

Ce jeudi 5 novembre 2026, la famille de Jesús de Miguel est sortie du silence après la disparition tragique de l'attaquant du CD Tenerife. Devant les médias, son représentant Manuel Hernández a relayé un message poignant de ses proches.

L'attaquant espagnol, âgé de 30 ans, est décédé le mardi 3 novembre 2026 des suites d'un violent choc au thorax survenu en plein match de deuxième division face au FC Andorra. Selon le rapport d'autopsie préliminaire transmis aux autorités judiciaires, le décès est consécutif à un traumatisme thoracique accidentel intervenu lors d'une sortie du gardien adverse Jesús Owono, exclu sur l'action. Une onde de choc pour le football espagnol.

Rassemblés dans la province de Tolède pour affronter cette perte soudaine, les proches du joueur ont tenu à saluer les nombreuses marques d'affection reçues depuis quarante-huit heures. Devant les médias, Manuel Hernández a souligné l'immense gratitude de la famille De Miguel Alameda envers les coéquipiers de l'attaquant, ses amis, les supporters ainsi que les clubs du monde entier qui ont multiplié les hommages. Une solidarité précieuse dans l'épreuve.

Au-delà de ces remerciements, les proches de l'attaquant ont formulé un appel clair au respect de leur douleur. La famille a en effet insisté sur la nécessité de préserver son intimité afin de pouvoir traverser cette terrible épreuve à l'écart de l'agitation médiatique.

Le recueillement s'organise désormais au sein du village natal de Navahermosa. La messe de funérailles de Jesús de Miguel a ainsi été fixée ce jeudi 5 novembre 2026 à 15h00 en l'église paroissiale San Miguel Arcángel. Un dernier hommage solennel pour accompagner le joueur entouré des siens.

La disparition tragique de l'attaquant a suscité une vive émotion jusqu'au sommet du football espagnol. Avant le transfert du corps vers la péninsule, plusieurs personnalités sont venues se recueillir à la chambre funéraire Santa Lastenia à Tenerife. Le président du Real Madrid Florentino Pérez et le directeur des relations institutionnelles Emilio Butragueño ont notamment tenu à faire le déplacement pour manifester leur soutien aux proches et au club insulaire.

Sur le plan sportif, les compétitions passent logiquement au second plan. D'après la presse espagnole, le match de la 13e journée de Liga Hypermotion entre le Granada CF et le CD Tenerife, initialement programmé le samedi 7 novembre 2026 au stade Nuevo Los Cármenes, a été reporté à une date ultérieure. À en croire ces mêmes sources, le CD Tenerife aurait également décrété six jours d'arrêt total de ses activités et prévoirait d'ouvrir les portes du stade Heliodoro Rodríguez López ce samedi pour un hommage populaire.

#2477. Se pensant hors antenne, Bertrand Latour révèle avoir gagné de l'argent en pariant sur les 10 buts inscrits du match Lens - Côme !

Pensant être hors antenne après la rencontre rocambolesque entre le RC Lens et Côme, le journaliste de Canal+ Bertrand Latour s'est retrouvé piégé par un micro resté ouvert. Une confidence inattendue sur ses gains aux paris sportifs qui n'a pas manqué de faire réagir sur les réseaux sociaux.

Ce mercredi 4 novembre 2026, à l'occasion de la 4e journée de la phase de ligue de la Ligue des champions, le RC Lens a sombré sur sa pelouse du stade Bollaert-Delelis face aux Italiens de Côme (1-9). Une déroute sans précédent dans la prestigieuse compétition européenne, marquée par un scénario fou qui a manifestement profité à certains observateurs.

La séquence s'est produite lors de la reprise d'antenne sur le plateau de l'émission « Canal Champions Club », présentée par Hervé Mathoux sur Canal+. Alors que le programme reprenait son cours, les micros ouverts ont capté les échanges privés de Bertrand Latour avec les consultants présents à ses côtés, à savoir Olivier Dacourt, Laure Boulleau et Walid Regragui.

Toutefois, cette scène n'a pas été diffusée sur les antennes françaises de la chaîne cryptée. C'est en effet sur les flux internationaux retransmis hors de France que les téléspectateurs ont pu entendre les propos du journaliste en direct, avant que l'extrait ne soit largement relayé sur les réseaux sociaux. Un couac technique.

Se croyant à l'abri des antennes, Bertrand Latour a révélé avoir tenté un coup particulièrement audacieux au cours de cette soirée européenne. Le journaliste a ainsi précisé à ses collègues avoir misé la somme de 10 euros sur « 9 et 10 buts » dans la rencontre opposant les Lensois aux Transalpins.

Une inspiration payante au terme d'un scénario surréaliste. Grâce à cette avalanche de buts, Bertrand Latour a confié avoir remporté un gain de plus de 500 euros. De quoi provoquer l'étonnement de ses interlocuteurs sur le plateau, surpris par cette confidence.

En revanche, la soirée a été bien plus compliquée pour le RC Lens. En s'inclinant sur ce score fleuve de 9 à 1 face à Côme, le club artésien a en effet concédé la plus lourde défaite à domicile de l'histoire de la Ligue des champions. Ce revers historique relègue désormais la formation nordiste à la 33e place du classement de la phase de ligue.

Il faudra rapidement réagir pour chasser les doutes. Le prochain rendez-vous du RC Lens est fixé au dimanche 8 novembre 2026 à 20h45 au stade Bollaert-Delelis face à l'Olympique de Marseille, pour le compte de la 10e journée de Ligue 1.

#2476. La rete di Pinamonti in Lazio-Cagliari: l'analisi dell'ex arbitro Marco Gabriele

L’ex arbitro Marco Gabriele analizza a livello regolamentare la rete di Andrea Pinamonti contro il Cagliari

L’ex arbitro Marco Gabriele è intervenuto ai microfoni di Radio Radio per esaminare il discusso gol di Andrea Pinamonti, arrivato sul finale di Lazio-Cagliari. L'episodio continua a far discutere poiché la rete del 2-1 è nata mentre Deiola era rimasto a terra dopo una pallonata, con il gioco che è proseguito regolarmente fino alla conclusione vincente dell'attaccante.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

A mio avviso Colombo ha gestito correttamente la situazione e la rete è regolare. Dopo la conclusione di Dele-Bashiru, il pallone è rimasto per alcuni secondi a disposizione dei giocatori del Cagliari, prima che l’azione proseguisse. Questo è un elemento importante nella valutazione dell’episodio.

L’arbitro non è obbligato a interrompere automaticamente il gioco ogni volta che un calciatore viene colpito dal pallone. Lo deve fare quando ritiene che ci sia un infortunio grave. Per questo, secondo la mia valutazione, Colombo ha fatto bene a lasciar proseguire l’azione.

#2475. « D’habitude, j’ai beaucoup à dire pour défendre notre équipe, mais pas ce soir » : Ruben Amorim accable ses joueurs après le fiasco de l'AC Milan

Quatre jours après le succès dans le derby milanais, l'AC Milan a sombré à domicile face à Ferencváros en Ligue Europa. Excédé par le visage affiché par son équipe, l'entraîneur portugais Ruben Amorim a recadré ses joueurs en conférence de presse d'après-match.

Le jeudi 5 novembre 2026, San Siro a été le théâtre d'une immense désillusion pour les Rossoneri, battus (0-2) par la formation hongroise lors de la 4e journée de la phase de ligue. Une prestation indigne des ambitions lombardes qui place l'AC Milan dans une situation délicate sur la scène européenne.

Face aux journalistes à l'issue du coup de sifflet final, le technicien portugais n'a pas mâché ses mots au moment d'analyser la copie rendue par ses hommes. Ruben Amorim a notamment qualifié la première période d'« affreuse », refusant catégoriquement de chercher la moindre circonstance atténuante à ce défaite devant le public milanais. Le ton est donné.

Pourtant galvanisé par sa prestigieuse victoire face à l'Inter (3-1) en Serie A le week-end précédent, le club lombard a déçu. Ruben Amorim avait procédé à une large rotation dans son onze de départ, où seuls Ramos, Rabiot, Musah, De Winter et Diawara avaient débuté le choc face aux Nerazzurri. Les Milanais ont craqué au retour des vestiaires sur une grave erreur de contrôle du gardien de 21 ans Lorenzo Torriani, aligné pour suppléer Mike Maignan suspendu, sur une passe en retrait du défenseur Koni De Winter sous la pression de Kristoffer Zachariassen. L'attaquant haïtien Lenny Joseph en a profité pour ouvrir le score à la 52e minute, avant de signer un doublé à la 66e minute.

L'expulsion directe de Yusuf Bamidele à la 77e minute pour un coup de coude sur Mario Gila a laissé Ferencváros en infériorité numérique pour le dernier quart d'heure, sans que Milan ne parvienne à réduire l'écart. Les entrées en jeu en cours de rencontre de Diego Moreira, Christian Pulisic et Luka Modric n'ont pas permis aux Milanais d'inverser le cours du match. En zone mixte après la rencontre, Koni De Winter a d'ailleurs partagé le constat général : « Ce sont encore ces moments de perte de concentration. Aujourd’hui (hier), rien n’allait du tout. Je ne peux pas l’expliquer. Tout est une question de mental : la façon dont nous abordons les matchs, la façon dont nous jouons. C’est notre point faible ».

Au-delà de la défaite, la situation comptable devient particulièrement alarmante pour les Milanais, qui pointent désormais à une inquiétante 28e place au classement général de la Ligue Europa avec seulement 3 points en quatre rencontres, soit une victoire et trois défaites. Pour tenter de redresser la barre, Ruben Amorim a insisté sur le manque de culture de travail et a érigé Strahinja Pavlović en modèle d'engagement, estimant que tout le monde devrait s'entraîner comme lui.

L'AC Milan tentera de réagir en Serie A lors de son prochain déplacement sur la pelouse du Genoa, programmé le dimanche 8 novembre 2026 à 15h00 au stade Luigi Ferraris.

Toutefois, Zinédine Zidane va pouvoir compter sur le retour de son capitaine Kylian Mbappé. L'attaquant devrait d'ailleurs retrouver une place dans le couloir gauche, où Zinédine Zidane envisage de le faire évoluer. Pour entourer le capitaine, l'ossature offensive de son premier rassemblement reste en place puisque Bradley Barcola, Désiré Doué et Michael Olise conservent la confiance du staff.

L'attaquant de l'Inter Milan apporterait sa puissance et son expérience internationale. D'ailleurs, une autre piste d'expérience est évoquée ces dernières heures. Florian Thauvin postule également à une convocation grâce à ses performances en club avec le RC Lens, où il totalise 6 buts et 8 passes décisives en 12 matches. Si son âge de 33 ans pourrait freiner Zinédine Zidane, son apport chiffré reste notable.

Au-delà des cadres confirmés, le staff technique garde un œil très attentif sur la jeunesse pour dynamiser les ailes. Mathys Tel fait ainsi partie des pistes suivies par les Bleus. L'attaquant de Tottenham représente une solution pour apporter de la percussion et de la polyvalence sur les ailes. Un profil offensif séduisant. Dans le même temps, Éli Junior Kroupi suscite également l'intérêt de l'encadrement tricolore. Déjà pré-sélectionné, le joueur de Bournemouth est apprécié par le staff des Bleus, même si son retour de blessure et son manque de rythme pourraient retarder son arrivée.

Ces choix en attaque s'inscrivent dans le cadre des deux dernières journées de la phase de poules de la Ligue des nations de l'UEFA 2026-2027. Dans le groupe A1 de la Ligue A, la France est en tête avec 10 points devant l'Italie qui compte 7 points. L'équipe de France se déplacera pour affronter l'Italie le jeudi 12 novembre 2026 à 20h45 à Milan, à San Siro. Les Bleus recevront ensuite la Turquie le dimanche 15 novembre 2026 à 20h45 à Bordeaux, au Matmut Atlantique, deux rencontres qui seront retransmises en direct sur TF1.

Toutefois, les joueurs olympiens n'auront pas le loisir de savourer longtemps cette victoire acquise en Allemagne. Le calendrier national impose en effet un retour immédiat aux affaires courantes, avec une affiche particulièrement attendue dans le nord du pays. Un test d'envergure en perspective. Le prochain rendez-vous de l'OM est fixé au dimanche 8 novembre 2026 à 20h45 sur la pelouse du RC Lens au stade Bollaert-Delelis pour le compte de la 10e journée de Ligue 1.

L'Italie accueillera la France le jeudi 12 novembre à Milan (20h45 sur TF1), avant la réception de la Turquie le dimanche 15 novembre à Bordeaux (20h45 sur TF1).

#2471. Lucas Hernandez de retour en équipe de France ? Zidane y pense...

Ce vendredi 6 novembre à 18h00, Zinédine Zidane annoncera sa deuxième liste à la tête de l'équipe de France depuis le siège de la FFF. Confronté à une avalanche de blessures, le sélectionneur tricolore s'apprête à repenser son secteur défensif... avec le retour de Lucas Hernandez ?

Pour aborder ce rassemblement capital en Ligue des nations, le staff technique des Bleus doit composer avec une cascade de forfaits majeurs dans ses lignes arrières. En effet, William Saliba n'est pas disponible pour cette échéance internationale, le défenseur central étant tout juste revenu d'une blessure au dos. Un coup dur auquel s'ajoute l'absence prolongée de Maxence Lacroix. Forfait en raison d'une déchirure aux ischio-jambiers, le joueur pourrait même être absent jusqu'à la fin de l'année. Enfin, Jules Koundé est également annoncé indisponible pour ces rendez-vous de novembre, n'étant pas encore remis de son entorse de la cheville gauche. Un casse-tête défensif.

Face à ces défections d'envergure, Zinédine Zidane explorerait plusieurs solutions d'expérience et de polyvalence pour rebâtir son arrière-garde. D'après L'Équipe, Lucas Hernandez et Castello Lukeba feraient ainsi partie des joueurs pressentis pour créer la surprise dans le secteur défensif de cette deuxième liste. Le joueur du Paris Saint-Germain sort d'une période positive avec la formation parisienne et offre une solution très appréciable dans l'axe ou à gauche. De son côté, Castello Lukeba possède un registre similaire. Le pensionnaire du RB Leipzig présente également l'avantage tactique de pouvoir évoluer dans l'axe central ainsi qu'au poste de latéral gauche. Une opportunité idéale pour les Bleus.

Au-delà de ces deux recours polyvalents, un autre profil inattendu pourrait créer l'événement dans le couloir gauche de la défense tricolore. Selon Foot Mercato, le latéral gauche Dayann Méthalie, qui évolue actuellement à Sunderland, a également reçu une pré-convocation de la part du staff des Bleus. Reste à savoir si le sélectionneur validera définitivement cette option ce vendredi à 18h00. Cette équipe de France renouvelée sera rapidement mise à l'épreuve lors des deux derniers matches de phase de poules de Ligue des nations. Les hommes de Zidane défieront l'Italie le 12 novembre à Milan à 20h45, avant de retrouver la Turquie le 15 novembre à Bordeaux à 20h45.

#2470. Official: Paul Nebel extends contract with Mainz 05

As officially announced by the Bundesliga club this afternoon, Paul Nebel has extended his contract with 1. FSV Mainz 05.

The midfielder, who joined the club at Bruchweg as a 13-year-old, progressed through all the youth teams at Mainz's academy.

#2469. « Je ne me sens coupable de rien » : Jorge Jesus réagit à la décision de Cristiano Ronaldo

Présent ce vendredi 6 novembre 2026 en conférence de presse à la Cidade do Futebol à Oeiras pour dévoiler sa liste de 28 joueurs, Jorge Jesus a tenu à clarifier la situation autour de Cristiano Ronaldo. Le sélectionneur portugais a en effet révélé n'avoir appris la retraite internationale de son attaquant que le jeudi 5 novembre 2026, au moment de la publication de sa vidéo et de son communiqué officiel.

Ce vendredi, la Cidade do Futebol à Oeiras a été le théâtre d'une annonce particulièrement attendue. Avant que Jorge Jesus ne présente sa convocation pour les rencontres de Ligue des nations face au Danemark et au pays de Galles, la vidéo d'adieu de Cristiano Ronaldo a été diffusée dans l'auditorium, précédée d'un hommage de Pedro Proença, le président de la Fédération portugaise de football. Mais alors que le dirigeant fédéral était dans la confidence selon la presse portugaise, le sélectionneur national l'a découvert lui en même temps que tout le monde.

Le technicien lusitanien, sous contrat avec la Fédération portugaise de football jusqu'en 2030, avait initialement prévu d'intégrer l'attaquant de 41 ans dans son groupe de 28 joueurs. Jorge Jesus n'avait pas cherché à lui téléphoner en amont, persuadé que le joueur poursuivrait l'aventure lors de cette phase de poules. Une surprise totale. Face aux journalistes, l'entraîneur a raconté sans détour les coulisses de ce dénouement :

Cette annonce intervient un mois après de vives tensions au sein du vestiaire portugais. Lors du précédent rassemblement, Cristiano Ronaldo avait reproché à Jorge Jesus de ne pas l'avoir fait entrer face à la Norvège, avant de s'agacer de l'absence d'excuses en conférence de presse et de claquer la porte. Interrogé sur d'éventuels regrets concernant la gestion de cet épisode, le sélectionneur a balayé tout remords : « Rien. Zéro. Parce que j'ai pris une décision d'entraîneur. »

D'ailleurs, le sélectionneur a tenu à évacuer toute idée de conflit personnel avec celui qui met un terme à sa carrière internationale après 23 ans sous le maillot portugais, cumulant 234 sélections et 146 buts. « Si j'ai dit que j'envisageais de le convoquer, c'est parce que je pensais qu'il allait poursuivre le projet de Ligue des nations. Il n'y a aucune animosité », a rappelé le patron de la Seleção, assurant ne porter aucune culpabilité dans cette issue.

C'est un changement majeur pour la sélection portugaise. Afin d'assurer la continuité sportive et symbolique, Jorge Jesus a confirmé que le brassard de capitaine revenait à Bernardo Silva, joueur le plus capé du groupe présent, conformément à la tradition de la Seleção. Au-delà de ce passage de témoin, le sélectionneur a profité de ce rassemblement pour rajeunir son effectif. Ainsi, deux nouveaux éléments font leur apparition pour la première fois : le latéral droit Alberto Costa (FC Porto) et l'attaquant Gustavo Varela (Monza).

L'équipe nationale doit désormais se focaliser sur ses prochains rendez-vous sur le terrain. Le Portugal recevra le Danemark le samedi 14 novembre 2026 à 20h45 à l'Estádio Municipal de Braga pour la 5e journée de la Ligue des nations, avant d'affronter le pays de Galles le mardi 17 novembre 2026 à 20h45 au Cardiff City Stadium.

#2467. « C'était pareil avec Vettel et Sainz » : Toujours en colère, Hamilton règle ses comptes avec Leclerc chez Ferrari

À São Paulo avant le Grand Prix du Brésil, Lewis Hamilton a haussé le ton contre Charles Leclerc. Toujours marqué par le scénario du Grand Prix du Mexique, le pilote britannique a pointé du doigt l'agressivité de son équipier chez Ferrari, coupable selon lui d'en faire bien trop contre sa propre monoplace.

Dimanche 1er novembre 2026, la Scuderia Ferrari disposait d'une occasion en or sur l'Autódromo Hermanos Rodríguez. Parti en pole position aux côtés de Charles Leclerc, qualifié au troisième rang, Lewis Hamilton espérait concrétiser la domination des monoplaces italiennes. Mais dès les premiers virages de Mexico, les deux partenaires se sont livrés une passe d'armes acharnée roue contre roue. En virant trop large, les deux monoplaces ont quitté brièvement la piste, manquant de peu l'accrochage. Sanctionné d'une pénalité de cinq secondes pour avoir quitté la piste et conservé un avantage durable lors de son dépassement, Charles Leclerc a vu Max Verstappen (Red Bull) s'intercaler et rafler la victoire. Lewis Hamilton a terminé deuxième et Charles Leclerc quatrième. Un immense gâchis.

Cette passe d'armes a provoqué la fureur immédiate de Frédéric Vasseur. Le directeur de la Scuderia Ferrari a fustigé la manœuvre au micro de Canal+ : « Ce n'est pas le résultat qu'on espère, surtout quand on est premier et deuxième après le premier virage. À Mexico, il n'y a pas eu un dépassement depuis 72 ans donc on n'espère pas finir deuxième et quatrième... J'ai du mal à l'avaler car je pense que c'est un manque de respect pour la marque, pour tous les gens qui travaillent, les efforts qu'on fait. » Le dirigeant n'a pas caché sa colère pour les 2 000 salariés de l'écurie après la perte de cette victoire promise. Charles Leclerc a d'ailleurs reconnu son erreur au micro de Canal+ : « La pénalité est clairement méritée. On ne fait tous les deux pas le virage, on freine tous les deux trop tard. Mon objectif était de redonner la position à Lewis mais Max (Verstappen) s'est intercalé entre nous donc ça devenait difficile. On va en discuter. »

Présent devant les journalistes à São Paulo, Lewis Hamilton n'a pas éludé le sujet. Le pilote britannique a accusé publiquement Charles Leclerc d'afficher une agressivité ciblée contre lui en piste, bien supérieure à celle réservée à leurs adversaires de Red Bull, McLaren ou Mercedes. Interrogé sur le comportement du Monégasque, Lewis Hamilton a dressé une comparaison sans détour avec les saisons passées de la Scuderia : « Je vois la même chose que vous. À Singapour, il était parfaitement [à la hauteur de Verstappen], roue contre roue, totalement à l'intérieur pour la prise de tête au virage 1 ; et je pense que c'était vraiment une situation différente. Si l'on regarde ses coéquipiers, c'était pareil avec Sebastian (Vettel) et avec Carlos (Sainz) », a lâché Lewis Hamilton.

Le Britannique a affirmé être toujours frustré et agacé par la manœuvre de son voisin de garage, alors que l'écurie italienne avait les armes pour s'imposer en Amérique centrale. Il a ainsi prévenu qu'une explication franche attendait son équipier : « J'étais évidemment frustré et je le suis toujours. On doit avoir une discussion et on l'aura en temps voulu à notre retour en Europe. Nous avions l'occasion de gagner pour l'équipe qui a fait un boulot incroyable en nous tirant vers le haut cette année. »

Toutefois, les deux hommes n'ont pas encore pu échanger de vive voix au Brésil. Charles Leclerc était en effet absent de la journée médias à São Paulo le jeudi 5 novembre 2026 en raison de la naissance de sa fille. L'ambiance s'annonce tendue dans le garage transalpin alors que Ferrari aborde la dernière ligne droite de la saison. Au classement des constructeurs, l'écurie italienne occupe la deuxième place avec 126 points de retard sur Mercedes, dont le pilote Kimi Antonelli a été couronné champion du monde des pilotes au Mexique. Au classement des pilotes, Lewis Hamilton figure au troisième rang, deux points derrière Max Verstappen, tandis que Charles Leclerc est cinquième.

Au-delà des rancœurs nées à Mexico, la Scuderia doit impérativement se remobiliser pour sécuriser ses positions au championnat. La prochaine course est le Grand Prix de São Paulo sur le circuit d'Interlagos, dont le départ est prévu le dimanche 8 novembre 2026 à 18h00 heure française (14h00 heure locale).

#2466. Le projet fou de Thomas Jolly pour Zinédine Zidane !

Dans le numéro 241 du magazine So Foot paru en novembre 2026, Thomas Jolly a révélé le projet initial imaginé pour Zinédine Zidane lors de la cérémonie d'ouverture des Jeux olympiques de Paris 2024. Le directeur artistique prévoyait en effet une séquence spectaculaire au-dessus de la capitale.

Pour marquer les esprits au début du spectacle, Thomas Jolly avait initialement prévu de faire apparaître un hélicoptère dans la nuit parisienne. Une échelle devait descendre de l'appareil afin que Zinédine Zidane puisse s'y accrocher, flamme olympique à la main, pour survoler Paris jusqu'aux jardins des Tuileries.

Cette mise en scène aérienne avait été conçue comme un hommage direct à Jean-Paul Belmondo. Le directeur artistique souhaitait en effet rappeler sa célèbre cascade dans le film Le Guignolo, réalisé par Georges Lautner en 1980. Un clin d'œil cinématographique assumé.

Si cette cascade suspendue au-dessus de la ville pouvait sembler particulièrement périlleuse, Zinédine Zidane n'a pas hésité à valider ce plan. D'après les propos de Thomas Jolly dans les colonnes de So Foot, Zidane avait en effet donné son accord pour réaliser cette cascade aérienne lors de la cérémonie.

Toutefois, cette arrivée aérienne n'a jamais pu se concrétiser. Malgré l'accord donné par Zinédine Zidane, le cadre réglementaire est rapidement venu bloquer la mise en place de ce tableau pensé pour la soirée inaugurale.

Le projet a finalement dû être abandonné en raison de strictes contraintes de sécurité et des règles d'interdiction de survol d'un hélicoptère à basse altitude au-dessus de la capitale. Face à ces exigences, Thomas Jolly a dû renoncer à cette séquence.

Privé de cette séquence dans le ciel parisien, Thomas Jolly a donc revu le rôle de Zinédine Zidane pour la cérémonie. Le directeur artistique a imaginé un autre parcours afin de mettre en valeur sa présence au cœur de l'événement.

Lors de la cérémonie d'ouverture officielle du 26 juillet 2024, Zinédine Zidane est finalement apparu dans le film d'introduction. Il est ensuite monté sur la scène du Trocadéro sous la pluie pour transmettre la flamme olympique à Rafael Nadal.

#2465. Exciting young talent JJ Gabriel makes debut as Man Utd beat Atletico Madrid

JJ Gabriel showed glimpses of his potential as he made his Manchester United debut in their 2-1 win over Atletico Madrid in Stockholm.

The 15-year-old may have been introduced with only eight minutes remaining, but, given how highly rated he is, it was a significant move by Michael Carrick to give him game time in the senior team.

There were hints of why Gabriel is so well regarded during those eight minutes, as he found space in the centre of the pitch and linked well with Leny Yoro. But he was also on the receiving end of some physical challenges – something he will have to grow accustomed to the more he plays at senior level.

Yoro, who himself made his senior debut as a 16-year-old for Lille, was impressed by Gabriel and said: “He’s a really good player. He is still really young and still has a lot to learn and a lot to improve on but he’s really humble and still working, so I’m happy for him to get his first minutes. I hope, for him, it’s going to continue like that.”

United fought back after conceding an early goal against Atletico to secure their second win of pre-season.

They fell behind in the sixth minute when Arnau Ortiz’s cross into the box evaded everyone, including United keeper Tom Heaton before hitting the back of the net.

United got the equaliser their performance deserved when the impressive Shea Lacey showed his skill in the box and was fouled. From the resulting penalty, Bryan Mbeumo sent the goalkeeper the wrong way from the spot.

In the 72nd minute, Mbeumo got his second of the afternoon after he bundled the ball home after the United press forced Atletico into a mistake.

The Spanish side hit the post twice in the closing stages but United held on to win.

“We had a clear plan before the game, everyone stuck to it and made the game enjoyable,” Mbeumo said. “We work really hard during training for these games, the main goal is to be ready for the season starting. I have done great things on the right but also as a striker.

“I think I am more experienced on the wing but I enjoy playing as a striker. For an offensive player, it is always good to score for the team.”

And it is. All over in Stockholm as United come back from behind to win. A really good performance by Michael Carrick’s side.

Iker Luque shoots from distance. It looks good but flies over the bar. And that should be the final effort of the match...

Mendoza and Hjulmand both hit the post in quick succession. Heaton was beaten on both occasions. United were a bit fortunate there.

Now that they are behind, Atletico are starting to have a bit more possession and play on the front foot.

A slow second half comes alive as United take the lead. A slack pass in defence by Atletico is pounced upon by Fletcher. He runs to the byline, cuts the ball back to the unmarked Mbeumo, who taps in.

OFF: Shaw, Dorgu, Lacey, MaguireON: Armer, Amass, Zirkzee, Williams

Big save from Heaton to deny Atletico at the near post after Yoro was beaten by a lovely pass from Lemar.

The energy and intensity of the first half has largely disappeared in this half. Neither keeper has been worked.

Another player, who has been tidy for United is Santos in midfield. He’s controlled the middle of the park, been safe in possession but also willing to fire a pass into the forwards.

Seven changes coming for Atletico. Typical pre-season move.

Mbeumo takes responsibility, waits for the keeper to dive and strokes the ball into the bottom left corner.

Dancing feet from Lacey in the box and Castillo brings him down. The referee rightly points to the spot.

Atletico make three changes. I’ll get them to you shortly!

No time added on, so that is the end of the half. An intriguing 45 mins. United will feel they don’t deserve to be losing.

United fail to clear the ball and it falls to Arnau Ortiz, who forces a good save from Heaton. That would have been harsh on United.

Lookman has been very quiet for Atletico. Yoro has marked him out of the game but in truth, Atletico have given him nothing to work with.

Apart from the move that led to Atletico’s goal, they have barely been in United’s half. They are struggling to beat United’s press and keep the ball. United are losing but they are working very hard and playing well.

Great pressing by United leads to Amad winning the ball back. Yoro finds Lacey, who spins and goes down the right. He whips in a cross and Mbeumo rises to attack it but heads wide. Probably should have done better.

It is probably an easy comparison but Lacey has a bit of Phil Foden about him with his movement and touch. He looks promising.

An aggressive press from United as they look to deny time to Atletico. They are pushing for that equaliser.

Amad, Mount and Mbeumo all want it. Amad takes it and his low strike is deflected and looks certain to beat Oblak but the keeper reacts quickly to make the stop.

Santos punches a pass through the lines, Mbeumo combines with Mount and the attacker is brought to the ground on the edge of the box...

Nearly a quick reply by United. Amad sets the ball for Mount to cross into the box. The midfielder’s ball goes to the far post, where Shaw meets it with a thumping volley but Oblak stands his ground and palms the ball over the bar.

From the resulting corner, Yoro should do better with his header but his effort goes wide.

Early goal for Atletico. They take a short corner, Arnau Ortiz whips in a dangerous ball that beats everyone and drops in at the far post. Heaton never moved.

United dominating possession in the opening stages with Atletico happy to sit off and let the Premier League side have it. Even in pre-season, Diego Simeone has his side set up well to defend.

The visit to the Strawberry Arena means United are back at the stadium where they won the 2017 Europa League.

📸 Wayne Rooney, 2017 🏆#UEL | @ManUtd pic.twitter.com/HxZVn1mAEQ

Gianni Infantino is facing an ultimatum from Uefa: resign or face an emergency no-confidence vote.

It is understood all 55 of Uefa’s member nations would opt to oust Infantino in a vote of no-confidence if he refuses to step down. Uefa can trigger such a vote with just 43 member nations.

European football’s governing body announced a loss of confidence on Saturday morning and has demanded change after Infantino was forced to scrap his £15bn plan to sell a stake in the World Cup.

Uefa’s statement followed widespread opposition and a revolt within Fifa’s ranks that staff are calling “kill the monster”.

The Football Association backed Uefa’s stance, saying: “We fully support Uefa’s position. It is time for a full and robust review of Fifa’s leadership and governance to ensure that the global game is run transparently, for the benefit of all 211 member nations and with the long-term stewardship of football at its heart.”

Good afternoon and welcome to pre-season coverage of Manchester United against Atletico Madrid.

Michael Carrick’s squad continue their preparations for the new season with a trip to the Swedish capital, Stockholm, where they face Diego Simeone’s La Liga side.

Last week, United cruised to a 5-0 victory over Rosenborg but today will be their first real test. They are slowly starting to get players back but the likes of captain Bruno Fernandes and Youri Tielemans will not be involved.

“I think we’re in a good place. I think there’s four weeks to go to the start and we’re gradually getting some boys back from the World Cup as we progress,” Carrick said.

“But I think a little bit of patience and time and trying to keep ahead and as I said, keeping everyone fit but moving forward is a really good direction to go in. And as it stands right now, I think we’re in a pretty good place. So, the boys will get better as we go.”

One player who will be looking to impress is Mason Mount. He has had an injury-hit spell at Old Trafford but is fit and ready to go for the season. He is also growing into a leadership role at the club.

He told BBC Radio Manchester: “You have different types of leaders, you have ones that are very vocal. I always want to see myself as one that leads by example and never stops working on the pitch.

“I’ve been captain previously years ago. I love the role especially with a lot of younger players on the pitch being able to speak to them and make them feel comfortable in the game and off the pitch as well. It’s a role that I really like and loved having it [the captain’s armband] in the second half.”

#2462. Lazio, Lotito valuta il rinnovo anticipato di Gattuso

#2458. Dowman weaves his magic and new signing shines as Arsenal hit ground running

Teenage sensation Max Dowman was the star of Arsenal’s first pre-season match, as he scored one goal and assisted another in a 4-1 victory over Girona.

Dowman, 16, was joined on the scoresheet by new signing Christos Tzolis, who struck with a low effort in his first game since his £34m move from Belgian side Club Brugge.

Established attackers Kai Havertz and Gabriel Jesus also scored for the Premier League champions, who were largely comfortable against opposition from the Spanish second tier.

Here are four things Telegraph Sport learnt from the match:

It was this time last year that Dowman first announced his remarkable talent to the wider world. Then only 15, he sparkled in pre-season and made it clear to Mikel Arteta that he was ready to impact matches at the highest level.

A year on, Dowman is now an established first-team player. He is the youngest ever Premier League winner and the competition’s youngest ever scorer, and he looks set to make even more of a mark this season.

In this friendly against Girona, Dowman created Arsenal’s opening goal with a gorgeous defence-splitting pass for Havertz. He did so after receiving the ball under pressure and spinning into space. Dowman then scored Arsenal’s third with a powerful low drive from the edge of the box.

Dowman is in competition with Bukayo Saka and Noni Madueke for a place on the right wing, which means this summer represents a huge opportunity for the teenager. After playing for England at the World Cup, Saka and Madueke will both miss most of pre-season.

Dowman, therefore, has a serious chance of going into the new campaign as the starting right-winger. The Community Shield on August 16 must be a target in his mind.

“We know what he’s capable of doing,” Arteta said. “We want to be more consistent in terms of the performances and the actions that he can deliver, but I think today he had a really good game.”

Arsenal are close to completing a deal for Newcastle United captain Bruno Guimarães but, for now, their only outfield signing of the summer has been Tzolis. This was the Greek winger’s first match in front of supporters and he immediately demonstrated why Arsenal were willing to pay £34m to buy him from Club Brugge.

With Tzolis, it is all about the final product. Goals and assists. He struck 22 times for Club Brugge last season, and registered 29 assists.

How did he produce such impressive numbers? The answer could be seen in his first-half goal against Girona. Receiving the ball on the left wing, Tzolis quickly accelerated towards the penalty area and unleashed a low shot. It was perhaps a more direct approach than Arsenal would usually have seen from Leandro Trossard, the player Tzolis has replaced, and it caught Girona off-guard as the ball deflected into the net.

“I’m very pleased with him,” Arteta said. “First of all, the way he has adapted to the club, to the team. The connection he’s already generating with the boys. I think you’re going to love the player.

“A player who’s very direct. He’s got the goal between the eyes. He has a lot of similarities with Leo [Trossard] in a lot of the things that he can do and the spaces he can occupy. And then the work rate that he has... He’s a boy that comes with so much hunger. He wants to prove himself, it’s a dream for him. And we are here to help him.”

Havertz was one of the few Arsenal players who benefited from playing at the World Cup. His injury issues mean that, unlike most of his team-mates, he did not end the 2025-26 season in a state of exhaustion.

After returning to the pitch in the spring, Havertz enjoyed an impressive end to the campaign for Arsenal. He then performed well at the World Cup, scoring three goals for Germany.

He remains one of Arteta’s favourite players, not least because of his versatility. He started in attack in the crucial end-of-season games in May, but played in midfield against Girona. His low finish, from Dowman’s pass, represented his eighth goal in his last 12 matches for club and country.

Girona’s goal, scored by Arnau Martínez, was perhaps the only alarming moment of this comfortable pre-season victory. Martínez was unmarked from a left-wing corner when he powered a header into Arsenal’s net.

For a group of players who pride themselves on their set-piece prowess, this was a disappointing moment. It also perhaps showed Arsenal’s reliance on their two first-choice centre-backs, William Saliba and Gabriel Magalhães, when they are defending crosses into their box.

Gabriel arrived at Arsenal’s pre-season training camp a few days ago, so he should be in good shape by the start of the new campaign. Saliba, though, will be missing for months with a back problem.

Without Saliba’s height and power, will Arsenal be as effective from set-pieces in the opening chunk of the campaign? Coventry City, Arsenal’s opponents on the first night of the season, will undoubtedly look to test the champions in these situations.

The reigning Premier League champions are handed the Costa Brava Trophy, which is half-heartedly lifted into the air by White. There will be far bigger trophies on offer to Arsenal in the upcoming campaign.

Arsenal have cruised to a routine 4-1 win over Girona, with Havertz, Tzolis, Dowman and Gabriel Jesus all finding the net. Arteta will be delighted with his side’s professionalism and ruthlessness.

Meslier takes too long on his goal kick, gifting a corner to Girona. However, the delivery from the hosts is so poor as it flies harmlessly out of play. That sums up Girona’s difficult evening.

There is another round of substitutions for both sides.

Arsenal’s makeshift line-up, which is full of Hale End graduates, is holding firm, with Girona evidently struggling for attacking inspiration. If Arsenal can see out this victory without any injuries, then it would have panned out exactly how Arteta would have wanted.

Girona carelessly give possession away in defence, allowing Nelson to dribble towards the penalty area. The winger floats a cross into the box, which is met by O’Neill, but the forward’s lofted header hits the top of the bar.

There is cooling break in this second half to give both sets of players a quick breather. It’s been a professional performance from Arsenal, who now have various academy prospects on the pitch. Can any of them follow in Dowman’s footsteps and make an eye-catching impression in this match?

Dowman was the breakout star of pre-season last year, when he was only 15, and he has started this campaign in similarly thrilling fashion. One assist, one goal. As you would expect, he looks bigger and stronger.

Arsenal overturn the ball in midfield and it breaks perfectly into the Brazilian’s path. Gabriel Jesus is one-on-one and makes no mistake, applying a devastating finish to make it 4-1 and put the result beyond any doubt.

The 16-year-old prodigy now has a goal and an assist. Dowman picks up the ball in the right attacking pocket, palms off Moreno, who bizarrely throws himself to the ground holding his face, before firing a resounding effort into the bottom corner. He’s such a talented footballer and looks at home in Arsenal’s first team.

Out of nothing, Girona have pulled a goal back. Kim whips a corner into the box, with the unmarked Martinez slamming a magnificent header into the top corner, leaving Kepa rooted to the spot. Arteta will be furious with his side’s complacency there.

The team known as “Set Piece FC” looked alarmingly vulnerable at that Girona corner. Perhaps this is simply the reality of life without William Saliba and Gabriel Magalhaes. The good news for Arsenal is that Gabriel is back with the squad and should be ready to go for the start of the campaign. The bad news is that Saliba’s back injury will rule him out for months. Will that lead to more sustained vulnerability over the opening chunk of the season?

Girona have sent a host of players out to warm up. They’re playing in their fourth pre-season match, with their league campaign due to start on August 16 against Leganes.

We are back under way for the second half.

That was a straightforward half for Arsenal. Arteta will be pleased with Havertz’s showing in his 33 minutes on the pitch. After missing so much of last season through injury, it has been a good summer for Havertz so far. He scored three goals in Arsenal’s final six games of 2025/26, and then netted four times for Germany in June.

The 2009-born playmaker was Arsenal’s standout player in that first half. Dowman’s confidence and class on the ball has been brilliant, with his expertly-crafted assist for Havertz’s opener an obvious highlight.

Arsenal are in complete control here in Spain thanks to first-half strikes from Havertz and Tzolis.

Dowman dribbles into the penalty area with ease, drifting past a couple of defenders before he is apprehended by Moreno. He goes down, but there is very little in the challenge.

Arsenal are in cruise control here. Girona are chasing shadows. Unsurprisingly, there’s a real gulf in class and quality between the two sides.

Once again, Dowman dances away from Moreno. The youngster sets up White with a reverse pass, but the Arsenal captain’s disappointing cross drifts out for a goal kick.

Tzolis registered 22 goals and 29 assists for Club Brugge last season. He is an end-product machine, and that goal was a little indication of how he racks up such eye-watering numbers. After receiving possession on the left wing, he was so quick to shift the ball onto his right foot and fire the shot at goal. He describes himself as a “direct” player and that was evidence of it.

There are three early changes for Arsenal, with Gyokeres, Havertz and Hincapie taken off for Gabriel Jesus, Vieira and Salmon.

With England wingers Bukayo Saka and Noni Madueke not returning until later in pre-season, Dowman has a chance to take ownership of the right wing position ahead of the Community Shield. His assist for Kai Havertz, a lovely measured pass after an excellent piece of skill in midfield, was a sign that Dowman means business.

Dowman drifts inside and pops up in the right channel, wriggling past Moreno, before threading a delightful pass through to Havertz. One-on-one, the German calmly slots a close-range effort into the bottom corner, giving Arsenal a well-deserved lead. So much credit must go to Dowman, who has started this game excellently.

Girona have some sustained possession to take the sting out of the game then burst into Arsenal’s penalty area, with the ball attempted to be pulled back to Van De Beek. The visitors have good numbers defending and comfortably deal with the danger.

It also appears that Havertz and Nwaneri have been tasked with quite free-roaming attacking midfield roles, with Lewis-Skelly positioned as a one-man holding pivot. It’s a strong but experimental line-up from Arteta.

After suffering an injury at the end of last season and missing the World Cup, White returns to action here this evening and wears the armband. He will look to support Dowman, who appears to be starting on the right wing.

It’s been a confident start from Arsenal, who are stroking the ball around in a very assured manner. Havertz looks to pull the strings by firing a low cross towards Gyokeres, but the delivery is scrambled clear by the Girona backline.

White leads Arsenal out onto the pitch to face Girona, who boast the likes of Van de Beek, Gil and Moreno in their starting XI.

Talking earlier this week, Arsenal boss Mikel Arteta sent out a determined message to other clubs by outlining their ambitious transfer plans.

“A lot is happening,” Arteta said. “We all know about the context of this transfer window. From ownership to the board, the sporting director and myself, we know that we want to take this club to a different level.

“That is going to require a better squad, better individuals. We have identified where we have opportunities to grow and to improve and to evolve our game and what is going to be needed to achieve that. Hopefully very soon we can start to put things in a very concrete way.”

Arteta also believes that last season’s Premier League title has served as added motivation for his squad to improve and hit new heights.

“This has to be the start and this has to be the standard,” he said. “Excellence has to be our standard and this has to drive to attack the future. The way we behave daily with more and more determination and ambition, because we’ve shown that we’ve got it. Now we have to show that we belong to this level and we want to increase and take this football club to a different dimension.”

It had been a relatively quiet transfer window for Arsenal, who have recruited just goalkeeper Illan Meslier and winger Christos Tzolis.

However, Arsenal are now moving very bullishly, closing on Newcastle United midfielder Bruno Guimarães, while also targeting Real Madrid’s Vinícius Júnior.

#2457. Paderborn vs. Eintracht Frankfurt preview: Die Adler searching for third successive win

Competing in the top flight for the first time since 2019-20, Paderborn are sitting in 14th spot after winning two, drawing three and losing three of their eight league games. The second of those victories came in their most recent home outing, a 1-0 success over Hamburger SV on October 24.

However, they have failed to score since Rayan Philippe netted an 88th-minute winner against Hamburg. They fell to a 1-0 defeat to Stuttgart in the DFB-Pokal, before they played out a goalless draw in last Saturday’s away clash against Borussia Mönchengladbach.

While the goals have dried up, Paderborn can at least be pleased with the fact they have avoided defeat in each of their last three league games (W1, D2). However, the league’s second-lowest scorers know they will need to improve in the final third if they are to put distance between themselves and the drop zone, with Ralf Kettemann’s side currently sitting two points clear of the bottom three.

They can take some confidence from the fact they have won all three of their previous home meetings with Frankfurt, even if the most recent of those encounters took place nearly seven years ago in December 2019.

Mario Götze is unlikely to feature due to an ear infection, denying him the chance to face off against his brother Felix Götze. Hütter is also without Jessic Ngankam, while Robin Koch is a doubt due to a knee problem.

Frankfurt head into the weekend in eighth spot after winning three, drawing two and losing three of their eight Bundesliga matches. Die Adler took maximum points from last Sunday’s home clash against Hamburger SV, just a matter of days after beating the same opponent by the same 3-1 scoreline in the DFB-Pokal.

The league encounter was level at one goal apiece at half time before Raphael Onyedika and Younes Ebnoutalib found the net to fire Adi Hütter’s side to all three points. After enjoying a successful outing at Deutsche Bank Park, Frankfurt will now head on their travels with the minimum objective of avoiding a third consecutive away defeat in the Bundesliga.

Their ultimate goal will be to record their second away win of the season, although that would require them to beat Paderborn at the Home Deluxe Arena for the very first time. While a head-to-head away win has evaded them up until this point, they were victorious in the most recent meeting between the two sides in June 2020, when they held off a Paderborn fightback to clinch a 3-2 win.

The visitors are expected to be without Marcel Hoffmeier, Nyamekye Awortwie-Grant and Timur Gayret. In positive news, Sven Michel is available for selection after recovering from the injury that has kept him out of the last two matches.

#2450. Signori e la Nazionale: «Quella ferita non si chiuderà mai», ritorno in azzurro

Il nuovo incarico nell’Under 20 allevia il dolore degli anni difficili; dopo la chiamata, il primo pensiero è andato a Tina.

Beppe Signori torna in azzurro: Malagò gli ha affidato il ruolo di capo delegazione della Nazionale Under 20. L’ex attaccante, che attendeva da tempo una chiamata nel calcio, si prepara al debutto del 12 novembre a Padova contro il Portogallo. Il nuovo incarico riporta con sé l’entusiasmo per una maglia indossata da protagonista e il ricordo degli anni segnati da una vicenda giudiziaria che ha condizionato profondamente la sua vita.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

In vista della partita contro il Portogallo, Signori descrive sensazioni diverse da quelle vissute da calciatore: «È un debutto diverso, ma per certi versi molto più emozionante. Sono carico di entusiasmo, sento una grande responsabilità».

Alla proposta di Malagò, racconta, non ha mai pensato di rispondere negativamente. La considerava un’opportunità attesa a lungo e non avrebbe voluto rinunciarvi per orgoglio o per la rabbia legata a ciò che aveva subito: «Assolutamente mai! Era una grande opportunità, l'aspettavo da tanto».

Signori, intervistato da Il Resto del Carlino, vuole partire da determinazione, sacrificio e passione. Intende aiutare i giovani a comprendere la responsabilità di rappresentare la propria nazione e l’orgoglio di vestire l’azzurro. A suo giudizio, oggi i ragazzi sono abituati ad avere tutto, mentre la sua generazione doveva conquistarsi ogni cosa.

Alla domanda se l’azzurro abbia perso fascino dopo tre Mondiali mancati, risponde senza esitazioni: «No, l'azzurro non passa mai. Quella maglia ti dà una sensazione forte, unica». Ricorda la competizione per un posto ai suoi tempi, quando tra gli attaccanti c’erano anche Baggio, Zola, Mancini, Vialli e Casiraghi. Per questo ritiene che i giovani debbano riconoscere il privilegio di giocare per l’Italia e dimostrare di esserne all’altezza, nonostante le distrazioni esterne e una società orientata, secondo lui, al tutto e subito.

#2449. Careless Liverpool concede same goal twice in second-half capitulation against Leeds

Leeds United came from 2-0 down at half-time to beat Liverpool 4-2 at Soldier Field in Chicago as Andoni Iraola’s side were undone by sloppy defending in the second period.

Liverpool’s dominant first-half display was underlined by goals from Luke Chambers and Florian Wirtz. However, Daniel Farke’s men replied with four strikes in 25 second-half minutes through Sean Longstaff, Brenden Aaronson and two alarmingly similar goals from Dominic Calvert-Lewin.

🎯 "CALVERT-LEWIN ARRIVES!" pic.twitter.com/CaHOeXQn7E

🤩 "Calvert-Lewin makes it FOUR!" pic.twitter.com/mEEi882OWi

Liverpool centre-back Chambers, 22, who spent last season on loan at Charlton Athletic, opened the scoring on seven minutes, pouncing on a Dominik Szoboszlai corner which had been flicked on at the near post by Milos Kerkez.

Jeremie Frimpong, serviced by a wonderfully flighted cross-field ball by Trey Nyoni, then set up Wirtz to make it 2-0 five minutes before the break, but on the hour Leeds began their recovery when Aaronson scored following a mistake by Kerkez – who gave up possession to Ethan Ampadu in his own half.

GOAL! Aaronson pulls one back! 2-1 pic.twitter.com/tdy17cITSd

Ampadu’s long throw was flicked on to Calvert-Lewin to level the score, then minutes later Curtis Jones’s carelessness allowed Longstaff in to put Leeds in front.

🔥 "LONGSTAFF! WHAT A TURNAROUND!" pic.twitter.com/G9LvLIk0Mi

And it was a familiar story five minutes from time when Ampadu’s throw again prompted chaos in Liverpool’s back line, allowing Calvert-Lewin to steal in front of Jones and seal victory.

Liverpool return to Anfield for friendlies against Monaco and Como before they begin their Premier League season away at Newcastle United later this month.

Leeds have matches against RB Leipzig, Manchester United and Augsburg before their league campaign gets under way at Nottingham Forest.

July 25- Leeds 2 Wrexham 3July 30- Leeds 1 Sunderland 0Today- Leeds 4 Liverpool 2August 8- Leeds vs RB LeipzigAugust 12- Leeds vs Manchester UnitedAugust 15- Leeds vs Augsburg

Possession: Liverpool 57%-43% LeedsShots: 9-15Shots on target: 3-7Corners: 3-7

The final whistle is blown in Chicago and that has well and truly been a game of two halves. At half-time, Liverpool were in complete control with a 2-0 lead. However, four Leeds goals in 25 second-half minutes have given Daniel Farke’s side the victory at Soldier Field. Both sides will now return home to the UK.

Aaronson is booked for a cynical pull on Ramsay.

Koumas should score for Liverpool. He is played in behind and for some strange reason Cairns comes a long way out of his goal. Koumas should lift it over Cairns and into the empty net but gets his chip wrong and the shot goes off target.

Leeds’ second half gets even better and Liverpool’s gets even worse. It is a very similar goal to Calvert-Lewin’s first; Ampadu’s long throw is flicked on and Calvert-Lewin meets it, heading past Mamardashvili. This is very much your game of two halves.

Daniel Farke is making a double change as Gnonto and Lienou come on.

Frimpong kicks the ball out of play and he may have a problem. Is it just cramp or more serious than that? He is going to come off and be replaced by Scanlon.

Leeds have come from 2-0 down to lead 3-2 in Chicago. Aaronson’s cross from the left side of the box should be cleared by Jones but he does not put his foot through it. Instead, that allows Longstaff to slot home past Mamardashvili. Leeds have scored three goals in a quarter of an hour.

Leeds are on level terms. A long throw from Ampadu out on the left is flicked on at the near post and Calvert-Lewin has the easiest of tap-ins at the back post. Liverpool were in cruise control at 2-0 up at the break but not have not been at it since half-time.

Justin is played in behind down the left channel and tries to pick out Calvert-Lewin in the centre of the box but Ndiaye steps in with a crucial interception.

Andoni Iraola is going to make a quadruple change. Elliott, Ramsay, Ndiaye and Tsimikas all come on. Szoboszlai is one of those to head off and hands Jones the armband.

Liverpool were much the better side in the first half but have not really been at the races since the break.

Leeds punish Kerkez’s error and get a goal back. Kerkez gifts the ball away inside his own half and Ampadu picks up not far outside the Liverpool penalty area. He spots the run of Aaronson to his left and slides it into the American, who takes one touch before sliding it underneath Mamardashvili and into the Liverpool net.

Wilson is fouled down Leeds’ right and he will take the free-kick. The Welshman clips it in and picks out Calvert-Lewin, whose header goes well wide of the target.

Calvert-Lewin gets a touch past Ndukwe just inside the Liverpool half and drives towards the Liverpool penalty area. It is difficult to know whether it is a shot or a cross from Calvert-Lewin and the ball goes wide of the far post. Bogle did make a run towards the back post and was hoping Calvert-Lewin would pick him out.

Liverpool negotiate a couple of long throws and a corner from Leeds in quick succession.

After Ndukwe’s pass inside his own half is intercepted, Justin decides to shoot from range but Mamardashvili easily saves.

Liverpool have made a number of changes at the break, with Chiesa, Jones, Gravenberch and Koumas all coming on. It takes an absolute age for Leeds to re-emerge, with Liverpool coming back out from the tunnel nearly ten minutes before Leeds. What on earth is going on in the Leeds dressing room at the break? This is ridiculous from Leeds.

The second half begins more than 20 minutes after the first ended. To be exact, 23 minutes is the gap. Calvert-Lewin is one of a few changes at half-time for Leeds, as is former Liverpool man Wilson. We are back under way in Chicago.

Jeremie 🔗 Flo 😍 pic.twitter.com/kxwhYA845Q

Andoni Iraola said before the game that Florian Wirtz would play 45 minutes in his first game of pre-season so we can expect him and Alexander Isak to come off at half-time.

Stateside Reds enjoying the action 🤩 pic.twitter.com/hfwaLmi8xB

Possession: Liverpool 60%-40% LeedsShots: 5-6Shots on target: 3-2Corners: 1-3

The first half is brought to an end and Liverpool take a 2-0 lead into the break courtesy of goals from Chambers and Wirtz.

Szoboszlai clips a delightful cross from the right all the way to the back post, aiming to pick out the run of Kerkez. A goal-kick is given, with the referee deciding the last touch came off Kerkez, but the Hungarian left-back is not happy with that decision. The replays prove why as the last touch came off Bogle.

There will be four minutes of added time at the end of this first half due to the Gudmundsson injury in particular.

Leeds have been forced into one change in this half and they might be forced into another here. As Wirtz scored, two Leeds players collided, which has resulted in Ampadu staying down and receiving treatment. The Leeds captain though does seem to be ok after receiving some treatment.

It is a lovely team goal from Liverpool to take them 2-0 up around five minutes before half-time. Nyoni’s flat ball out to the right side of the box is volleyed first time back across the box by Frimpong. Wirtz runs onto the ball and, from the penalty spot, slots the ball home past Cairns.

After Nyoni makes an interception inside his own half, Liverpool counter. The ball is shifted out to the left, where Ngumoha drives at Bogle. Ngumoha’s step-over takes him past Bogle inside the Leeds penalty area and he drives the ball across the six-yard box but no-one in a Liverpool shirt is on hand to tap it in. Should Isak have done a better job to get on the end of that one? Possibly.

Szoboszlai, who loves scoring free-kick stunners, blasts the free-kick into the wall.

Gudmundsson’s day is indeed over and is replaced by Bornauw, which could see a shift for Leeds.

Ngumoha is brought down by Bogle inches outside the Leeds penalty area out on the left. It was very, very close to being a penalty but was just outside the box. Before the free-kick will be taken, Leeds look like they will be forced into a change as Gudmundsson has gone down clutching at his right hamstring.

For the second time in a few minutes, Rodon steps in with a crucial interception. Wirtz slides a pass through into the Leeds box on the left to pick out the run of Ngumoha. Just as Ngumoha was getting his shot away from around eight yards out, Rodon slides in to intercept. However, the offside flag goes up against Ngumoha anyway.

Wirtz and Isak link up really well inside the Leeds half, with the latter putting a touch beyond one Leeds defender and runs towards the Leeds penalty area. However, Rodon steps in with a crucial interception to take it off Isak.

Nmecha scored from long range for Leeds the other day against Sunderland and he is not far off adding another long-range goal to his pre-season tally. His effort from just outside the box is dragged just wide of Mamardashvili’s right post.

Frimpong drives forward from inside his own half and takes Liverpool through the Leeds half before he is cynically pulled down by Tanaka, who is rightly booked. Sometimes in a pre-season friendly players might get away with that but this referee does not show mercy to Tanaka.

That is nearly a horror moment for Cairns in the Leeds goal. He rolls the ball out from his own box and Isak just about gets a touch on it but fortunately for Leeds it does not end in a horror show for Cairns.

Frimpong lays it across to Nyoni in a central position outside the box and the latter takes on the shot. Nyoni goes for accuracy over power and looks for the bottom corner but Cairns gets down to his left to make the save.

Chance for Leeds. Bogle whips in a cross from the right touchline and picks out the head of Nmecha, whose effort is saved well by Mamardashvili down to his right.

Liverpool hit the front in Chicago. Szoboszlai’s outswinger corner from the right is flicked on at the front post by Kerkez and is met by Chambers, who smashes home on the half volley into the Leeds net.

Liverpool counter from their own box after defending a long throw through Isak and Frimpong, whose cross from the right is deflected behind for the first corner of the game...

The pitch is not looking in the best condition, which perhaps is not a massive surprise when you consider that Chicago Fire played here last night. They beat Charlotte 2-1, with Robert Lewandowski scoring twice.

Gudmundsson is back to his feet after some treatment but is breathing quite heavily. It does appear that the Swedish wing-back should be fine to continue.

After Liverpool clear a Leeds long throw into the Liverpool penalty area, Gudmundsson goes down clutching at his lower back after an awkward landing and play is halted.

We are under way at Soldier Field. It is not too hot in Chicago today, with the temperature a touch over 20 degrees at kick-off.

Both sides emerge from the tunnel and we are just moments away from kick-off at Soldier Field in Chicago. You’ll Never Walk Alone rings around the stadium as the teams emerge.

Liverpool: Mamardashvili; Frimpong, Ndukwe, Chambers, Kerkez; Nyoni, Szoboszlai, Wirtz; Morrison, Isak, Ngumoha.Substitutes: Woodman, Davies, Chiesa, Jones, Elliott, Tsimikas, Gravenberch, Abe, Ramsay, Scanlon, McConnell, Koumas, Ndiaye, Wright, Sonni-Lambie.

There was a lot to take in, during those madcap 42 seconds, but it was a scene that showed the full extent of the issues Andoni Iraola must address.

First came a handshake with Curtis Jones that the midfielder, lost in red mist, barely acknowledged. It was not impressive, shrugging off a new manager like that, but as Iraola strode across the field at Yankee Stadium, after Liverpool’s 1-0 win over Wrexham on Wednesday, the action really began.

Jones squawked and jabbed at his arm. Dominik Szoboszlai bellowed back, arms stretched out so wide you could not help but notice. Next came Kostas Tsimikas throwing the captain’s armband on the floor, landing at Jones’s feet, as Giorgi Mamardashvili and Jeremie Frimpong looked on awkwardly.

The French defender, who has arrived from Rennes this summer, is not involved for Liverpool today and is yet to feature for his new club so far this pre-season as he is eased in after a shoulder injury days after his move to Liverpool was confirmed earlier this year.

Both Premier League encounters between these sides last season ended in draws; a 3-3 draw at Elland Road was followed up by a 0-0 stalemate at Anfield. The draw at Elland Road was immediately followed by Mohamed Salah speaking to the media, claiming he had been thrown under the bus.

It is a new era at Liverpool as former Bournemouth boss Andoni Iraola has replaced Arne Slot at Anfield. They kicked off the Iraola era in Nashville, Tennessee, with a 4-2 win over Sunderland, following that up with another victory in New York on Wednesday night, beating Wrexham 1-0, and will be aiming to make it three wins from three on their US tour.

“I think it [the US tour] has been really, really useful,” Iraola said in an interview on the official Liverpool website. Obviously, you spend a lot of time with the players, with the staff, you get all the information you can. And I think there is also an evolution, it has to be. We start to understand each other better, what we need to do. Every game, I hope we can get closer and closer and put ourselves in a position where the few players that still are not here start to train also, they are helped by the others.”

Virgil van Dijk and Cody Gakpo resumed training back on Merseyside whilst Alexis Mac Allister, Alisson and Victor Munoz are yet to return to training following their World Cup exploits. The trio of Florian Wirtz, Alexander Isak and Ryan Gravenberch are all set to feature for the first time this pre-season.

Leeds’ pre-season campaign began with a 3-2 loss to Championship Wrexham in Tampa, Florida before beating Sunderland 1-0 in New Jersey courtesy of a long-range strike from Lukas Nmecha. Former Liverpool man Harry Wilson joined Leeds on a free from Fulham this summer.

“Really tough but that is expected,” Wilson said. That is what pre-season is for, building up a base for when the season starts, making sure you are in the best physical position you can be to attack the season. It has been good. I played against them [Leeds] twice last year when I was at Fulham. Both games were really, really tough games.

“I think when the fans have got a good team to support, I think that [the atmosphere at Elland Road] goes up a notch. I think the size of the club was a massive part of me coming. Then speaking to the guys, the chairman, the sporting director, and seeing kind of the plans and the aims for the club, it is something I wanted to be a part of.”

È un concetto anche che posso capire, figlio di un'esasperazione di anni in cui sono stati tanti i giocatori da 20 milioni (i Musah, i Loftus-Cheek, i Reijnders, perché poi non avevi preso solamente scarsi, e così via; i Chukwueze, che però è costato di più; i Pulisic, anche Pulisic è figlio di questa politica). Lo posso capire; non lo posso capire nel momento in cui però dalla fascia A – che è quella di Moreira e Gonçalo Ramos, lasciando perdere poi il rendimento di Gonçalo Ramos, ma questo è un altro discorso – si passa automaticamente a una fascia C che neanche in Europa League, e pensate in Italia, può bastarti".

Nel discorso, Letizia fa un nome: "Senza gettare la croce su nessuno, non è un gettare la croce personale: ma ragazzi, Filippo Terracciano è un ragazzo che l'anno scorso giocava nella Cremonese retrocessa e non eccelleva neanche. Oggi fa il titolare in Europa League al Milan. Capite che qualcosa non funziona? Che qualcosa, secondo me, non ha funzionato nella costruzione della rosa".

#2446. 2. Bundesliga: Dresden off the bottom with convincing victory over Fürth

First half goals from Robert Wagner and Ben Bobzien had the hosts firmly in control at the half. Vincent Vermeij added a second-half strike to make it 3-0 before Hendry Blank saw red for the visitors capping off a miserable evening for Fürth. Dresden made a lively start at the Rudolf-Harbig-Stadion, although Greuther Fürth initially looked dangerous going forward. The visitors came close to opening the scoring through Omar Sillah, whose effort drifted narrowly wide after a defensive mix-up, before Sacha Bansé tested Lennart Grill with a powerful effort from distance. Dynamo also threatened at the other end, with Patrice Čović missing a promising opportunity from inside the penalty area.

However, it was the hosts who eventually broke the deadlock in the 31st minute. Niklas Hauptmann did well to turn away from two opponents before laying the ball into the path of Robert Wagner, whose first-time effort took a deflection off Hendry Blank and wrong-footed goalkeeper Florian Hellstern.

Dynamo doubled their advantage ten minutes later, with Ben Bobzien finding the net in similarly fortunate circumstances. After receiving the ball from Tobias Raschl, the forward cut inside before seeing his effort deflected by Blank and loop into the top corner, leaving Hellstern with little chance of making a save.

Fürth almost reduced the deficit before the interval, with Sillah narrowly missing a cross from Gian-Luca Itter before the latter saw two efforts blocked and deflected wide. Despite those opportunities, Thomas Stamm's side headed into the break with a deserved two-goal advantage.

The hosts continued to apply pressure after the restart and effectively put the contest beyond doubt in the 55th minute. Bobzien turned provider this time, setting up Vincent Vermeij, who had the simple task of rolling the ball into an unguarded net to make it 3-0.

Fürth's evening then went from bad to worse seven minutes later when Blank received his marching orders. Having already been booked in the opening minute, the defender brought down Bobzien as he threatened to break through on goal, receiving a second yellow card and leaving the visitors with ten men for the remainder of the contest.

Dresden looked increasingly comfortable against their numerical disadvantage, with Čović coming close to adding a fourth in the 73rd minute, only for his effort to be blocked by Krisztián Keresztes close to the goal line.

The hosts ultimately saw out a convincing 3-0 victory, recording another impressive home result following their recent wins over Magdeburg and in the cup against Schalke. The three points also provide a significant boost to Dresden's hopes of climbing away from the bottom of the 2. Bundesliga table, while Fürth will be disappointed with an evening in which defensive misfortune and Blank's dismissal contributed to a heavy defeat.

#2443. Generali Stadium, avvertimento a Inter e Milan: Majorino apre alla revisione, Calabresi vuole ridiscutere

Generali Stadium, avvertimento a Inter e Milan: Majorino apre alla revisione, Calabresi vuole ridiscutere

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Il progetto del nuovo Generali Stadium di Inter e Milan torna al centro del dibattito politico milanese. A riaccendere la discussione sono le dichiarazioni di Pierfrancesco Majorino e Mario Calabresi, entrambi tra gli aspiranti candidati sindaco del centrosinistra in vista delle elezioni comunali del 2027.

Scegli Fcinter1908.it come tuo sito preferito su Google: clicca qui

Due interventi distinti, ma con un punto in comune: la volontà di rimettere in discussione alcuni aspetti dell'operazione, qualora ci fossero le condizioni per farlo.

Il capogruppo del Pd in Regione Lombardia ha spiegato che entro la primavera del 2027 dovrebbero arrivare alcuni passaggi fondamentali, tra cui la sentenza del Consiglio di Stato e le valutazioni sull'impatto ambientale e sanitario dell'intervento.

A quel punto, secondo Majorino, sarà possibile capire quali margini esistano per intervenire sul progetto.

«Se ci fossero le condizioni per rimettere in discussione il progetto, sarebbe la cosa giusta da fare».

Majorino ha quindi precisato che, qualora l'operazione non fosse più modificabile dal punto di vista formale, diventerebbe fondamentale concentrarsi sulla tutela dei residenti, sulle conseguenze ambientali e sull'utilizzo delle risorse comunali per riqualificare il quartiere San Siro.

Non si tratta, dunque, dell'annuncio di un blocco già deciso, ma di una posizione che lascia aperta la possibilità di modifiche in base agli sviluppi amministrativi e giudiziari.

"Entro la prossima primavera, intanto, saranno chiarite alcune cose, ad esempio ci sarà già stata la sentenza del Consiglio di Stato e avremo visto le valutazioni di impatto ambientale e sulla salute. Se ci fossero le condizioni per rimettere in discussione il progetto, sarebbe la cosa giusta da fare. L’ho già detto mesi fa e non cambio idea. Io in Consiglio comunale mi sarei fatto sentire per cambiare quella delibera. Se, invece, la vicenda di San Siro non sarà modificabile dal punto di vista formale, bisognerà essere attentissimi alle questioni di impatto ambientale per tutelare i residenti del quartiere e sul modo in cui investire le risorse ottenute dal Comune per riqualificare la parte più popolare di San Siro”.

Il giornalista, che ha manifestato la propria disponibilità a partecipare alle primarie del centrosinistra, ha spiegato che, in caso di elezione a sindaco, intende incontrare Inter e Milan per affrontare nuovamente alcuni aspetti dell'operazione.

«Alla luce di quello che sta succedendo alcune cose vanno ridiscusse».

Calabresi ha individuato soprattutto tre questioni: la tutela della salute dei residenti durante i lavori, l'impatto ambientale della costruzione del nuovo impianto e della demolizione del Meazza e la necessità di garantire benefici concreti agli abitanti del quartiere.

Secondo l'aspirante candidato sindaco, le due società dovrebbero assumersi maggiori responsabilità nei confronti del territorio interessato dal progetto.

A suo giudizio, l'intervento dovrebbe essere sostenuto economicamente da Inter e Milan, senza ricadere sulle finanze comunali.

Va però precisato che, secondo il piano illustrato, il costo complessivo dello spostamento è stimato in 68,1 milioni di euro, a carico dei club, con una parte dell'importo coperta attraverso lo scomputo degli oneri di urbanizzazione.

Non sarebbe quindi corretto affermare che, allo stato attuale, l'intera opera sia finanziata direttamente dal Comune.

Il tema dello stadio resta così uno dei principali terreni di confronto politico in vista delle prossime elezioni milanesi.

Per Inter e Milan, che hanno già presentato il progetto del Generali Stadium, le posizioni di Majorino e Calabresi rappresentano un nuovo elemento di attenzione. Le dichiarazioni non modificano automaticamente gli accordi esistenti, ma confermano che il futuro dell'area di San Siro continuerà a essere al centro del dibattito cittadino.

"Il sindaco si è arrabbiato parecchio - ha ammesso Calabresi parlando della reazione di Giuseppe Sala sulle sue critiche sullo stadio -. Io ho detto tre cose, la prima che dobbiamo preoccuparci della salute del quartiere, dell'impatto ambientale della costruzione del nuovo stadio e della demolizione del vecchio. Oggi siamo in quella fase".

Il secondo punto "è che il quartiere non deve pagare un prezzo senza avere nessun tipo di beneficio, di ritorno", ha aggiunto e "lo dico senza che sia lesa maestà, voglio dire la mia". Ad esempio secondo Calabresi lo spostamento del tunnel Patroclo non deve essere pagato dal Comune ma dalle squadre.

"Allora dobbiamo accettare che tutto quello che è stato deciso debba rimanere immobile? - ha spiegato ancora -, le squadre vanno sfidate perché ci sia un impegno sul quartiere superiore". Sul fatto di non abbattere San Siro, "non la penso come La Russa perché dico cose che lui non ha detto - ha rivendicato infine -. Sullo stadio dobbiamo essere laici".

La Champions League è il massimo livello, quindi tutto deve essere migliore e più veloce. Le decisioni devono essere prese a un ritmo più elevato, e questa è l'esperienza che ci manca ancora come squadra. Dobbiamo assolutamente migliorare sotto questo aspetto."

"La preparazione per la prossima partita è semplicemente la stessa. Ovviamente, si impara dagli errori. Quando vediamo come l'Inter difende e sceglie i momenti per pressare, possiamo imparare molto come squadra. Spero che, come squadra, possiamo portare a casa punti importanti da questa partita"

Eintracht Braunschweig and Energie Cottbus drew goalless after an uneventful encounter at the start of matchday 11 in the 2. Bundesliga.

Sui social si è anche dibattuto della correttezza nel continuare a fare gol anche di fronte ad una partita con il risultato più che chiuso. Sul presunto fair play si è espresso in maniera netta Enzo Bucchioni: CONTINUA A LEGGERE.
