#2437. Mykhailo Mudryk returns as Chelsea slip to Juventus defeat

Mykhailo Mudryk made his first Chelsea appearance since November 2024 as Xabi Alonso’s team succumbed to their second pre-season defeat.

Mudryk replaced Nicolas Jackson in the 82nd minute against Juventus, marking his return to competitive football after a long doping ban that was resolved this week thanks to changes in Wada regulations.

There were a couple of crosses delivered from the left flank by the Ukrainian winger, who could yet leave Chelsea on loan or on a permanent basis before the close of the transfer window.

Juventus were the victors though, thanks to a sumptuous 68th-minute goal by Kosovo winger Edon Zhegrova.

Alonso had 21 players on his bench, and made 12 second-half substitutions.

Levi Colwill and Cole Palmer featured in the defeat by 10-man Spurs on Saturday, but sat this game out as they continue to build fitness. Danny Welbeck did start though, reunited with former Brighton team-mate Joao Pedro up top.

Chelsea scored just one goal against Spurs and Juventus, but the thrust provided by right-back Marco Palestra in tandem with first Geovany Quenda and then Estevao was a bright spot for Alonso.

Chelsea continue their pre-season against AC Milan on Saturday, before they host Real Sociedad at Stamford Bridge on August 15.

They begin their Premier League campaign at Fulham in the Monday night slot on the opening weekend.

Even Chelsea’s in-house streaming had given up the ghost by the end. Consecutive pre-season defeats for Chelsea, but worth remembering that Colwill and Palmer sat out this fitness exercise and Enzo Fernandez is still to return. Palestra’s performances a real positive but the team is still finding its way.

Juventus win a corner late on, but Yildiz’s delivery is too close to Penders. Just two minutes for Chelsea to find an equaliser.

Mudryk does well to dig out a cross from the left but the goalkeeper Perin gathers. There will be four minutes of added time to play.

Chelsea not really threatening an equaliser as the game meanders towards a conclusion. Should they score a goal, we will have a penalty shoot-out.

Estevao looked for Mudryk with a reverse pass between full-back and centre-back but there was too much weight on the ball.

Estevao’s free-kick is saved but the bigger news for Chelsea fans is that Mykhailo Mudryk has been introduced from the bench, replacing Nicolas Jackson for the closing stages.

Juventus goalkeeper Perin sells Nicolas Jackson a dummy so he can pick a pass into midfield. The Italian side controlling the game quite well since the goal, against a weaker Chelsea team.

Chelsea’s younger players will have to dig them out of trouble in the last 20 minutes. There has not been much in the game, Chelsea have shaded it in general play without quite creating enough.

The Kosovan winger with a first-time, left-footed hit from almost 25 yards, curled into the top corner. Nothing Penders could do about it.

More changes from Chelsea: Anselmino, Acheampong, Satpaev, Assugo and Delap the players on, replacing Hato, Palestra, Gittens, Caicedo and Pedro. Alonso finishing the team with his younger players on the pitch.

Jonathan David slides a pass across the Chelsea six-yard box when there was a chance to shoot, but the flag was up for offside in any case.

Penders does well to palm away an inswinging cross from Zhegrova, before Estevao picks out the run of Palestra on the break with a brilliant forward pass. Chelsea corner, cleared. Now Caicedo has been booked for a cynical challenge to stop a break, much like Douglas Luiz.

Nicolas Jackson hacked down by Douglas Luiz after he turned on halfway with three or four Chelsea runners in support. Painful one for Jackson, and a yellow card for the former Aston Villa midfielder. Chelsea players not happy with that challenge in a pre-season game.

Chelsea certainly have the upper hand in the game, quick feet from Joao Pedro to win a free-kick in midfield. If either team is going to score the game’s first goal, you fancy it will be them.

This time Chelsea find Palestra with a ball over the top, but his shot at goal is at a comfortable height for the goalkeeper.

Palestra popping up in a centre-forward position again, but his shot towards goal is not struck cleanly. The right-back at the heart of Chelsea’s attacking again.

Two very different XIs out there for the second half.

Penders replaces Sanchez in goal, Sarr on for Tosin, Estevao on for Quenda and Jackson on for Welbeck.

For Juventus: Bremer, Perin, Zhegrova, Owusu and David are on.

Not much to write/phone/WhatsApp home about, so far. The combination between Quenda and Palestra down Chelsea’s right has been the best feature of their football, am sure we will see Estevao on that flank in the second half among other changes. The bumpy pitch and a hydration break has not helped the spectacle.

There will be four minutes of stoppage time, simply a result of the hydration break.

Far too much space opens up between Chelsea’s defence and midfield, with Meritti turning and carrying the ball forward. Boga tests Sanchez with a strike from the edge of the box. That was probably Juventus’s most threatening attack.

Palestra defends well as the last man up against Milik, Chelsea almost undone by Locatelli’s outside of the foot pass.

Robert Sanchez caught under a cross swung to the back post, very unconvincing goalkeeping, but Juventus could not get their shot away in the box. Nicoll-Jazuli then did well to bring the ball away for Chelsea at the edge of the box.

Chelsea have another corner, and yet again it comes from a right-sided attack. Chelsea are in ascendancy now after a slow start, but it is a very low intensity contest.

Caicedo with a couple of threatening crosses from a centre-right position, but no Chelsea player can connect with them cleanly. Gittens’s dribbling down the left causing problems for Cambiaso, and he was the one attacking Caicedo’s cross at the back post.

Play has now resumed after the drinks break. No changes from either team, those will come at half time. Chelsea have 21 players sitting on their bench, albeit a number of academy players are on tour.

Time for a hydration break, even though the game is being played under a closed roof.

Chelsea’s right side is their most dangerous, by a distance. Quenda does well to slip Palestra in on the overlap, and his low cross into the six-yard box is cleared for a corner. Juventus clear. Boga with a burst through the Chelsea midfield but the Juventus man then gets his pass wrong.

Chelsea enjoying their most sustained spell of possession in Juventus’s half so far, without quite carving out a clear cut chance. Juventus defending their penalty area with a back five, but they do not have Bonucci, Chiellini and Barzagli back there anymore.

Will be interesting to see if they can find a way for him and Reece James to start games. Chelsea as a team yet to find much rhythm in the game so far.

Palestra with a loose first touch but he recovers and defends well. Like in Sydney, this looks another very bumpy pitch, you can see the join marks between the pieces of turf that have been freshly laid. Coming out of these games without any injuries will be a priority for Alonso.

Juventus get the game started in their pale pink away kit.

The wheels of commerce continue to turn for Chelsea in the transfer market but this afternoon Xabi Alonso’s team continue their preparations for the new season against Juventus in Hong Kong.

They have the chance to recover from the ignominy of losing to 10-man Tottenham Hotspur in Sydney on Saturday, but with 19 days until Chelsea’s Premier League opener against Fulham these matches are still a fitness building exercise. The positive from the Spurs game was Wesley Fofana, Levi Colwill, Cole Palmer completing 80 minutes – three players who endured injury problems last season.

Another new arrival is expected to follow Morgan Rogers, Marco Palesta, Maxence Lacroix, Jordan Henderson and Danny Welbeck to Stamford Bridge, with the club nearing a deal for Rayo Vallecano left-back Pep Chavarria.

It wouldn’t be Chelsea without talk of some eye-catching outgoings, with Spurs reportedly interested in Nicolas Jackson and Enzo Maresca eyeing a reunion with Pedro Neto at Manchester City.

Alonso’s team promise to be flexible in their tactical approach this season, but so far he has set up with a back four rather than the 3-4-2-1 system he used to such great effect at Bayer Leverkusen. That could change as availability increases closer to the start of the season, but Palestra’s bursts forward from right-back in tandem with Estevao have caught the eye.

Juventus finished sixth in Serie A last season under Luciano Spalletti, the former Roma and Napoli coach who usually guarantees expansive and attractive football. Turkey left-winger Kenan Yildiz was their top goalscorer last season, and it looks like they will make it through the summer without the left-winger being picked off by one of the Premier League’s elite. Yildiz scored 11 goals in all competitions, and Dusan Vlahovic was the only other player to reach double figures so scoring goals is a problem to address. Lois Openda and Randal Kolo Muani have been signed this summer to strengthen the forward line.

#2433. Il Milan non perdeva le prime due sfide europee a San Siro da 16 anni

Il Milan ha perso due partite casalinghe di fila in una singola stagione nelle competizioni europee per la prima volta dal 2010/11 (contro Ajax e Tottenham). Lo riporta Opta.

Ruben Amorim, allenatore del Milan, ha parlato in conferenza stampa al termine di Milan-Ferencvaros, sfida valida per la quarta giornata della Europa League Phase.

"Non si tratta della formazione titolare, si tratta della preparazione, di essere pronti mentalmente a vincere ogni partita e della responsabilità che abbiamo come club. Non importa chi scende in campo dal primo minuto, dobbiamo lottare per ogni seconda palla, curare i piccoli dettagli. Non parlo di tecnica, di fare magie o di vincere ogni singola gara: parlo delle piccole cose, di ciò che possiamo controllare. E oggi, specialmente nel primo tempo, non ci sono spiegazioni. Non ho nulla per difendere me stesso e i miei giocatori per la prestazione di oggi. La gestione dell'undici iniziale è legata anche al fatto che, se riproponiamo certi calciatori troppo spesso, rischiamo gli infortuni; devo gestire i minuti, devo immaginare come si svilupperà la partita, ma non voglio parlare della formazione titolare. Voglio dire che oggi non ci sono scuse per la nostra prestazione, soprattutto nei primi 45 minuti. Naturalmente lotteremo in questa competizione fino alla fine, ma la verità è che sembra che non riusciamo a mantenere lo stesso livello a distanza di tre giorni, ed è su questo che dobbiamo lavorare."

#2431. Hosts HSV end losing streak against ten-man Gladabach

Outstanding goals helped Hamburger SV took all three points at home against bottom-placed Borussia Mönchengladbach on Friday night action in Bundesliga.

HSV took the lead after a frantic start at both ends for ten minutes. Gladbach had decent chances via Shuto Machino and Lukas Ullrich, while there was a penalty-kick shout for a challenge on Albert Grønbæk before Patson Daka failed to penalize Joe Scally's mistake.

In contrast, the goal came from a low probability of conversion rate as Miro Muheim fires one from long range. Including his late winners against Hoffenheim and Stuttgart, the 28-year-old scored three goals in only five Bundesliga appearances after scoring once in the past two campaigns.

Daniel Batz and Daniel Heuer Fernandes then made decent saves to deny Martin Adeline and Zento Uno as the end-to-end nature of the game produced an enthralling encounter. Despite their positions in the standings, the former giants followed an attacking approach in front of Günter Netzer, who won the Bundesliga twice with Gladbach before transforming HSV in his role as general manager after retirement.

Although the onrushing Heuer Fernandes was targeted by Florian Neuhaus’ ambitious attempt that followed with Merlin Polzin throwing instructions, the hosts dominated the remaining of the half as the next big chance fell to David Möller Wolfe before a wild free-kick from close range by Grønbæk.

The two players also combined for another chance before Gladbach equalized against all odds before the half-time whistle. Joe Scally's effort didn't seem to be troubling for HSV initially, but it went all the way after a slight deflection by Robin Hack.

In his first start since matchday 4, Ko Itakura was the only player that went into the referee's handbook in the first 45 minutes. The Japanese international, who returned to the club this summer after only a year at Ajax, then saw his second booking with an unnecessary challenge four minutes after the restart.

Alexander Blessin immediately decided to introduce Kevin Diks to shore up his defense, but HSV scored with their first real chance of the half. Daka couldn't reach Grønbæk's pass in the box, but 19-year-old center-back Shafiq Nandja smashed it home with great composure for his first goal in professional football.

HSV still made life difficult for themselves as a miscommunication between Heuer Fernandes and Sebastian Bornauw invited an equalizing goal before the latter redeemed himself with a last-ditch block to thwart Scally. The American defender rued his missed chance as Hamburg found the third goal with another low xG attempt.

Grønbæk added a splendid goal to his third assist of the season. The Danish midfielder's first goal at Volksparkstadion was a thing of beauty as he found the back of the net with an unstoppable hit. The new Germany international Wael Mohya tried to brought the visitors back in the game, but it was too late for them as Itakura's sending off changed the complexion of the duel.

The hosts end a three-game losing run in all competitions, while Gladbach's surprising unbeaten streak similarly ended after three matches. Polzin's men momentarily climbed seven places before the rest of matchday 9 action in Bundesliga.

Hamburg will travel to fierce rivals Werder Bremen in the first Topspiel after the international break, while cellar-dwellers Gladbach entertain second-placed Borussia Dortmund.

L'Inter ha un nuovo padrone della fascia destra: il suo nome è Djed Spence. I problemi fisici post-Mondiale l'hanno rallentato e hanno messo in mostra Andy Diouf in quel ruolo, ma ora l'inglese si è conquistato la titolarità e non ha intenzione di mollarla. Scrive La Gazzetta dello Sport: "Detto che Spence non è Dumfries, c’è però qualcosa che accomuna i due. Non dal punto di vista tecnico (uno ha dribbling e l’altro forza fisica, uno ha velocità e l’altro qualità finalizzative), bensì a livello di inserimento in squadra. Djed, un po’ come il primo Denzel nerazzurro, in questa Inter ha faticato a ingranare per quei motivi fisici che hanno rallentato qualsiasi idea di impiego immediato di Chivu.

Ecco, Spence e Dumfries hanno in comune un primo tratto di strada in nero e azzurro un po’… dissestata. Denzel per la sua prima da titolare ha dovuto aspettare metà settembre e la quarta giornata, Djed la sesta. La media di minuti a partita in campionato del primo, nelle sue prime dieci giornate di Serie A interiste, era di 27,3; il secondo di appena 11. Ma in Champions League hanno iniziato entrambi a vedere la luce: destino ha voluto che tutti e due alla loro terza presenza europea (sempre nel primo anno in nerazzurro) realizzassero il loro primo assist in campo internazionale: l’olandese contro lo Sheriff, l’inglese al Feyenoord.

"Gli inizi all’Inter non sono stati facili per me – ha ricordato proprio DD in una recente intervista –, sono stato molto criticato. Io sono arrivato dopo una grandissima stagione di Hakimi, l’Inter aveva appena vinto lo scudetto e molte persone non mi conoscevano. In cuor mio sapevo che all’inizio non sarebbe stato facile ma dal primo giorno ho avuto il desiderio di dimostrare che ero un giocatore da Inter". L’obiettivo anche di Spence, che ora raccoglie l’eredità di Dumfries come quest’ultimo fece con Hakimi nel 2021. Passano gli anni ma il percorso non cambia, almeno lì a destra. E adesso anche Djed, dopo un iniziale scetticismo, è pronto davvero a prendersi l’Inter".

#2429. Genoa, De Rossi sul Milan: "Ha tanti campioni. Ieri ha perso? Sabato ha battuto la squadra più forte in Italia"

In vista del match contro il Milan di domenica alle 15 a Marassi, Daniele De Rossi, tecnico del Genoa, ha rilasciato queste parole in conferenza stampa come riporta tuttomercatoweb.com:

Cosa vi ha dato questa settimana? "Ci ha dato un po' più di tempo per preparare la partita. Un piccolo vantaggio in più rispetto al Milan. Abbiamo spinto un pochettino mentre la settimana scorsa abbiamo gestito i carichi. Siamo andati un po' più nel profondo per analizzare quello che vogliamo fare e quello che abbiamo fatto. E' questo il momento in cui le cose vanno analizzate, quando le cose vanno molto bene o molto male, e con un po' di tempo in più abbiamo toccato questi aspetti".

II Milan ha avuto picchi altissimi ma anche battute d'arresto come quella di ieri sera. "Lo abbiamo guardato sotto l'aspetto tecnico-tattico. Valuto quello che vedo in campo, i giocatori che hanno. E credo che siano una rosa tra le prime tre del campionato, sono molto forti e costruiti nel modo che a me piace tanto. Il derby lascia scorie positive anche se ieri hanno avuto una partita molto difficile".

Sono rientrati in gruppo Venturino e Havel. Nel ruolo di trequartisti ora hai scelta. "Vorrei portarli in panchina ma non con un ruolo di spettatori ma partecipi del gruppo. Sono due giocatori importanti per caratteristiche. Havel è una punta importante. Venturino può saltare l'uomo, ha gamba".

In alcune occasioni hai schierato tutta la qualità, in altre invece hai tenuto i colpi più tecnici in panchina a gara in corso. Hai già un'idea su come affrontare il Milan domenica? "Se non avessi idee a due giorni dalla partita sarebbe preoccupante (sorride ndr). Stiamo valutando sia l'aspetto tecnico che quello mentale e fisico dei nostri avversari. Valutiamo un po' tutto. Dobbiamo fare una partita seria. Non dobbiamo montarci la testa, siamo una squadra di medio-bassa classifica, sono i numeri che lo dicono non voglio sminuire il Genoa, e loro sono una grande squadra. Dobbiamo essere aggressivi ma consapevoli che affrontiamo una squadra che se siamo troppo spavaldi ci fanno male. Non dimentichiamo che il Milan ha battuto la squadra più forte in Italia".

Avrebbe preferito vincere incontrare un Milan reduce dalla doppia vittoria nel derby e in Europa o, come ora, con la vittoria nel derby e questa sconfitta in Europa League? "Forse vederli arrivare ubriachi di gioia da due vittorie poteva sembrare un piccolo vantaggio. Considerando i campioni che hanno nel loro spogliatoio saranno ancora più concentrati. Cambia poco. Parliamo di Modric o Rabiot, Maignan, Ramos e Pulisic e un allenatore che ha allenato il Manchester United. Non sono giocatori che se avessero vinto con il Ferencvaros sarebbero venuti a Genova per fare una passeggiata. La interpreteranno al 100% perché è così che fanno i campioni e loro lo sono".

#2428. ​Arteta’s ‘dynasty’ dream collides with Arsenal’s harsh reality

Mikel Arteta says he wants to create a “dynasty” at Arsenal as he threw down the gauntlet to his players to retain their Premier League title and reach a “different level”.

Arsenal are on the verge of completing a £75m deal for Brazil midfielder Bruno Guimãraes from Newcastle and have not given up hope of landing Real Madrid winger Vinícius Júnior, who has been offered an improved contract by the Spanish giants.

The Premier League champions slumped to a 3-1 defeat to Real Betis in Dublin on Wednesday night, when they paid the price for individual errors and a lethargic, sloppy defensive display.

But that did little to quell Arteta’s ambitions for the new season. “I’m even more excited [about this season], and we’re going to go and attack every possible title because that’s the ambition that we have,” he said.

“We know that we have the ability. We will go from a strength to a strength as well, whether it’s with the players that we have to take them to a different level, and with the market that is still open to recruit as well, so we are very optimistic about it.

“All the work that we’ve done – that I’ve done through the summer – is to understand what we need to do to win again and to create a dynasty in this football club. That’s the aim.”

Despite Arteta’s bullishness, he conceded Arsenal would have to be a lot better than they were against Betis. When asked about the mood in the dressing room, he said: “Very upset, given a hard time. They are fuming, but this is good. That’s what we need.”

Telegraph Sport analyses some of the talking points from a disappointing evening at the Aviva Stadium.

The importance of goalkeeper David Raya and centre-backs William Saliba and Gabriel Magalhães to Arsenal hardly needs overstating. Their title success was built on the foundations provided by that triumvirate at the back.

Raya has yet to report back from holiday after Spain’s World Cup triumph and Saliba is facing an extended spell on the sidelines with a back injury. Gabriel did appear for the second half but, watching this, Arteta is unlikely to have been dissuaded from the belief he needs another top defender to compensate for the loss of Saliba.

It may have been only Arsenal’s second game of pre-season, so a degree of legginess was understandable.

But the ease with which they were cut open and overloaded by the Spanish side, and the volume of individual errors, did not sit comfortably with Arteta with just over a fortnight until they kick off their title defence at home to newly promoted Coventry City.

Only once in 63 matches last season did Arsenal concede three goals in a single game but they were fortunate Betis did not score more. “In the first half there are two different things: to play well and to compete,” said Arteta, who saw his side trail 3-1 at the interval.

“We did the first one quite well in a lot of moments but the compete part – especially in and around our box – was not to the standards to win a football match against a good side. It’s better to give them [the goals away] in a friendly match than an official match. It’s always good when you lose a match in pre-season because it creates a bit more fire in the belly.”

Kepa Arrizabalaga will not want to watch Betis’s first goal back in a hurry. The Arsenal goalkeeper may have got a slight nudge as he came for the ball at a corner but he was all over the place and never looked even in the vaguest control of the situation. Rodrigo Riquelme scored at the far post after the ball sailed over the keeper. Arteta called it a “really sloppy goal”. With former Leeds goalkeeper Illan Meslier having arrived on a free transfer as back-up to Raya, Kepa faces an uncertain future at Arsenal. Moments like this will not help his cause.

He did not feature much last season but is probably best remembered for his torrid showing in the Carabao Cup final defeat by Manchester City in March. He looked as unconvincing here as he did then, as the former Chelsea goalkeeper also sliced a clearance in the build up to Betis’s third goal. Before then, Kai Havertz had turned over possession cheaply from which Nelson Deossa set up former West Ham midfielder Pablo Fornals to score.

Arsenal need more creativity and firepower in attack, hence the interest in Vinícius and Atlético Madrid’s Julián Alvarez. Viktor Gyokeres again looked rather limited here and the likes of Bukayo Saka and Eberechi Eze are also still to report back. Yet one of the few, brighter aspects of Arsenal’s performances was the sight of the regal looking Riccardo Calafiori marauding forward from a very nominal left-back role.

If Arsenal are going to play more adventurously this season and try to ratchet up things in the entertainment stakes then Calafiori could have a big role to play, as long as Arteta can keep him fit. He can be a potent weapon galloping forward and there were clear glimpses of that here. Harnessing that talent while not compromising too much defensive security may be one of the manager’s challenges.

Louie Copley had only been on the pitch for nine minutes – a half-time substitute for Ben White – when the 19-year-old midfielder went down largely unchallenged with what looked like a troubling knee injury. Arteta admitted afterwards that the way the teenager fell to the ground was a concern and he looked in visible pain and distress as he was treated by the medical staff before being helped off the pitch.

The fear will be of some kind of ligament damage and Arteta confirmed Copley would be scanned on Thursday. “Quite worried to be fair for the kid because the mechanism doesn’t look very positive and the symptoms that he already has,” the manager said. “So we are going to have to wait and see for tomorrow’s scan. Touch wood that is not a bad one.”

🌴 @RealBetis_en's Hector Bellerin reacts to a 3-1 preseason win over his former club Arsenal"I spent 10 years over there always trying to help and see Arsenal where it belongs, and it's nice to finally face them with them being in that position" 🗣️ pic.twitter.com/er3YtAysQb

“It is another opportunity for fitness. Coming into games against teams of good quality, it is important we get good fitness. It is a chance to build momentum for the season ahead. We are taking steps in the right direction. Of course, we want more from the performance and the results, but we take learnings and move on.

“All of us, together, we share a common goal. We have confidence to build on. We will take every game as it comes. The atmosphere was amazing. I did not realise Ireland has so many Arsenal fans. We are so grateful for everyone who came to support us today. We wanted to give them a good result and performance. Hopefully next time we can.”

“We always want to win and in the end we could not today. In some ways they deserved it and we could have done better. We did have some good moments though. We had an amazing season last season. I believe in this team and there are still players to come back from the World Cup. I am confident but we can always do better.”

There is the final whistle in Dublin and Betis secure a 3-1 win over Arsenal at Aviva Stadium. Goals from Riquelme, Deossa and Fornals has led Manuel Pellegrini’s side to the victory. All four goals in the game came before the break.

And that's full time in Dublin 👏Real Betis claim a 3-1 win against Premier League champions Arsenal 🌴 pic.twitter.com/0XWByNdtgh

Jesus gets onto the end of a ball in behind, getting to it just before the byline, and delivers a dangerous cross but Natan does well on the stretch to clear.

Antony was booed when he came on and is also being booed whenever he touches the ball.

Arsenal have registered just two shots on target in this game and both of those came in the first half. They have not really looked like scoring since the break, with probably Jesus coming closest.

Double change for Betis as Morante and Gomez replace Riquelme and Bernal.

Bellerin squares the ball from the byline and picks out Riquelme near the penalty spot but the latter is unable to take advantage.

The changes keep coming for Arsenal as Gabriel and Martinelli, who have only recently returned from their breaks after the World Cup, go off after half an hour on the pitch. Nwaneri was the final member of the Arsenal starting XI to still be on but his night is over.

Up until this point, Manuel Pellegrini had made just one change but he now makes a triple substitution, which sees three former Premier League players come on. Former Leeds duo Firpo and Llorente come on as does ex-Manchester United forward Antony. There are a few boos from the Arsenal fans as Antony comes on.

Martinelli is played in down the left channel and gets onto the end of the ball inside the Betis box. He checks back and finds his compatriot Jesus on the edge of the box. Jesus tries to pick out the bottom corner but his shot goes just wide.

Isco should put Betis 4-1 up but tries to be too cute and clever. Former Arsenal man Bellerin delivers a cross from the right side of the box and picks out a free Isco in the six-yard box. Isco attempts an audacious flick rather than putting his foot through it and his effort is saved by Meslier. That should have been a goal.

Arsenal have changed nearly their entire starting XI whereas Betis have made just the one substitution thus far. Betis have played more games so far in pre-season, which probably explains the difference in the number of changes.

Salmon is deceived by a dummy by Riquelme and then brings the Betis man down, earning him a yellow card.

On the hour mark, Mikel Arteta is going to make a triple change, which feels like it was a plan going into the game. That includes Meslier coming on in goal in place of Kepa.

Copley is helped off by the two Arsenal medics, shaking his head and not putting any weight on his left leg, and is replaced by Julienne.

You have to hope that the knee injury to Louie Copley is not serious but judging by the way he went down largely unchallenged, his reaction and the way he was being helped down the tunnel it didn’t look too good. A tough moment for the youngster who had only been on the pitch nine minutes.

Play is halted as Copley has been down for a little while clutching at his left knee. He got his studs stuck in the ground and went down in some pain. The medics come on to check that left knee. He only came on at half-time but his night is over not even 10 minutes into the second half.

Nwaneri catches Bernal and if this was a competitive fixture than I think Nwaneri would perhaps have been booked for that challenge.

Those raft of changes from Mikel Arteta at half-time have seen just four of their starting XI remain on the pitch; Kepa, Calafiori, Lewis-Skelly and Nwaneri.

Arsenal have a corner early on in this second half as Vieira’s shot from distance deflects behind. The delivery from the left has too much on it, going over the heads of all the Arsenal players in the box, and Betis are able to clear.

Mikel Arteta has made a whole host of changes, which includes the likes of Jesus, Martinelli, Gabriel and Vieira coming on. Betis have brought the former Real Madrid man Isco on at the break.

Possession: Arsenal 63%-37% Real BetisShots: 5-7Shots on target: 2-6Corners: 1-2

Arsenal’s title triumph was built on that stout defence and conceding so few but there have been so many self-inflicted wounds here. The first and third goals came from dismal individual errors from Kepa and Havertz and the second - while a wonder strike - came after Arsenal had failed to clear their lines properly.

The first half is brought to an end and Real Betis take a 3-1 lead into the break.

Manuel Pellegrini’s side have their third goal of the night and restore their two-goal advantage. Havertz gives the ball away poorly deep inside his own half and the ball is played into Fornals. The former West Ham man has it on the edge of the box and has P Garcia in space to his right but opts to shoot. It is a clinical finish from Fornals as he picks out the bottom left corner and celebrates by crossing his arms to form irons, paying tribute to his days at West Ham.

"It's so so easy!" 🎙️The former West Ham man Pablo Fornals puts Real Betis 3-1 up against Arsenal 🌴🔨 pic.twitter.com/iyBeyS1nHd

Betis come close to restoring their two-goal advantage. Calafiori gives the ball away deep inside his own half and the ball is quickly moved onto P Garcia on the right side of the box. He opens up his body and curls a shot first time towards the far corner, forcing Kepa into the save down to his right.

Riquelme shoots from just outside the box and forces Kepa into a save down to his right.

Betis have one goal from a corner and now Arsenal do. Betis keeper Valles complains that he is being fouled by Havertz after Tzolis swung in the corner from the left and the ball comes through to Hincapie, who has the simplest of headers from inside the six-yard box.

Normal service is resumed with a Gunners goal from the corner 👏Piero Hincapié gives the @Arsenal fans reason to celebrate! 🔴 pic.twitter.com/b7h979i0Gv

Important block from P Garcia. Dowman cuts in from the right across the face of the penalty area and finds Tzolis in space to his left. Tzolis takes one touch before taking on the shot from inside the Betis box but P Garcia slides in with a crucial block.

That is quite the finish! Deossa receives the ball in a central position around 25 yards out. He takes a couple of touches before lashing a shot with his left foot. His effort flies into the top corner and Betis have a 2-0 lead. Considering the quality of the finish, I am surprised that Deossa did not celebrate more bearing in mind you do not score screamers like that every day!

Nelson Deossa...what a hit! 😱😱Real Betis double their lead over Arsenal with a stunning strike and Mikel Arteta doesn't look happy 🌴 pic.twitter.com/4J5QnjOury

Since Gyokeres misses that very good chance just a minute into the game, Arsenal have not really carved out many genuine opportunities in front of goal, other than that Tzolis wasted chance. Every time they reach the final third, they are lacking that cutting edge.

Calafiori’s role is interesting. He’s very nominally a left back but is routinely stepping into midfield and raiding forward, often ending up in positions you’d associate with a No. 10. It’s the kind of role Nico O’Reilly played under Pep Guardiola at Manchester City.

Valles is so, so lucky. The Betis keeper inexplicably passes the ball straight at Tzolis inside the Betis penalty area but Tzolis is unable to capitalise. That is a huge slice of luck for Valles as that was a ridiculous moment.

Betis have already scored one goal from a corner and they now have another corner after Nwaneri’s clearance behind. The inswinger is sent into a good area but this time Arsenal deal with the danger.

Arsenal were the better side in the early stages but Betis have grown into this and have taken confidence from that goal.

The first goal of the night goes the way of the Spaniards. The corner from the left should be dealt with by Kepa but the Arsenal keeper makes an absolute mess of it. The ball goes straight through him and comes to Riquelme at the far post. He takes one touch before firing into the net. Kepa is appealing for a free-kick but there is never a foul in there.

Real Betis strike first at the Aviva! 🌴Riquelme takes advantage of poor Arsenal defending at the corner to open the scoring 👀 pic.twitter.com/1ubVDayElP

That is dire goalkeeping from Kepa. He’s desperately tried to claim he was fouled but he just made a complete hash of it.

Betis have their first shot of the night. P Garcia receives it out on the right and cuts inside onto his left. The space opens up for him to shoot and he obliges, forcing Kepa into a save before the ball is smashed behind for a corner...

Calafiori may be on the teamsheet tonight for Arsenal at left-back but when they are in possession the Italian is moving into midfield. Lewis-Skelly, who is playing in the number six role, is dropping in between the two centre-backs when Arsenal have the ball.

Betis win a free-kick out on the left, with the chance to send it into the Arsenal penalty area for the first time tonight. It is clipped into a good area but Kepa is out of his goal to catch it.

Arsenal should be ahead one minute into the game with their first attack of the game. Havertz picks up the ball on the corner of the box out on the left and passes it inside to Gyokeres. The Swedish striker takes one touch before shooting from the edge of the six-yard box but his shot is saved. In reality, Gyokeres should not have given Valles in the Betis goal any chance of saving that one.

Both sides emerge from the tunnel, Arsenal led out by Ben White and Betis by former Arsenal man Hector Bellerin, and make their way past the Premier League trophy. We are moments away from kick-off in Dublin.

Arsenal are closing in on the signing of Newcastle and Brazil midfielder Bruno Guimaraes in a deal worth £75m. Our starting XI for #ArsenalRealBetis! 💚🤍 pic.twitter.com/8rRvy9IZCm

🟡 𝗧𝗘𝗔𝗠𝙉𝙀𝙒𝙎 🔵 Unchanged from last time out.Let's keep our good pre-season form going ✊🤝 Presented by @deel pic.twitter.com/S5TKqR9Lv3

Premier League champions Arsenal continue their preparations for the new season as they take on Spanish side Real Betis at the Aviva Stadium in Dublin, with this being the first-ever meeting between the two sides. This is the first time Arsenal have played a pre-season game in Dublin since August 2018, when they drew 1-1 with London rivals Chelsea, with Arsenal winning on penalties.

Arsenal beat Girona 4-1 in their opening game of pre-season, with new signing Christos Tzolis on the scoresheet in his first appearance for the club. After today, Arsenal have three more games, including the Community Shield, before they kick off the defence of their Premier League crown at home against newly-promoted Coventry on opening night of the new season. They are closing in on a deal for Newcastle captain Bruno Guimaraes, who is set to become one of Arsenal’s most expensive signings. Mikel Arteta’s side have been dealt a blow ahead of the season with the news that France centre-back William Saliba will miss an extended period due to a back injury. The likes of Declan Rice, Bukayo Saka and Noni Madueke are still yet to return after their World Cup exploits.

Real Betis, under the management of former Manchester City boss Manuel Pellegrini, finished fifth in La Liga last season and have therefore qualified for this coming season’s Champions League after a 20-year absence from the competition. Pellegrini has led Betis into European competitions for five consecutive seasons, which includes losing the 2025 Europa Conference League final to Chelsea. They have been busy in pre-season action already having played five games up to this point. They have won four of the five, including a 4-0 victory over Lyon, with just one defeat coming against Granada.

They have a number of former Premier League players in their ranks, including Antony, Giovani Lo Celso, Pablo Fornals and former Arsenal man Hector Bellerin. Real Betis have already played a number of friendlies as they build up to the start of their season, which begins on August 21 when they take on Real Sociedad.

#2426. Cardone: "Zac out? Ci sarà Pinamonti dal 1' e Noslin a sinistra. Tavares è affaticato, ma..."

Le dichiarazioni del giornalista Giulio Cardone ai microfoni di Radiosei

Nell'intervento radiofonico di questa mattina il giornalista Giulio Cardone, giornalista de La Repubblica, ha commentato gli ultimi aggiornamenti in casa Lazio.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Tavaras è affaticato, però dovrebbe iniziare la gara per poi magare lasciare il campo a mezz’ora dalla fine.

La Lazio deve guardare con grande attenzione Napoli-Roma, lì loro hanno fatto una buona partita. Il Napoli domenica o crolla o si rilancia.

Cataldi e Rovella? Cataldi sta bene, non ha tutta la partita sulle gambe. Rovella andrà in panchina domenica, così come Marusic; sono stati recuperati, poi avranno tutta la sosta per lavorare bene. Gudmundsson dopo la pausa, da capire se già contro il Lecce.

Con molta probabilità giocherà Pinamonti, con Noslin che trasloca a sinistra ma non è l’unica opzione che sta valutando Gattuso.

Per quanto riguarda lo stadio Flaminio, io so che l’accordo è per una capienza di 47mila spettatori. La Lazio non era disposta a scendere sotto i 45mila. Ci sono altre situazioni da chiarire, ma al momento l’inter sta andando avanti. Parlano di incontri positivi, ma non credo arriveranno ulteriori risposte entro il 2026.

#2425. Ferencvaros, Nagy ammette: "Penso che questa sia stata una delle vittorie più belle, se non la più bella, della mia carriera a livello di club"

Ádám Nagy, centrocampista del Ferencvaros, intervistato da M4 Sport, ha rilasciato queste parole dopo la vittoria in Europa League contro il Milan: "Il fatto di non aver concesso loro spazio e di averli pressati nei momenti giusti li ha spiazzati. Credo che, considerando la partita che abbiamo giocato per novanta minuti, meritassimo di portare a casa i tre punti. Sono davvero molto contento. Se qualcuno mi avesse detto che sarei tornato a casa con una vittoria da San Siro non ci avrei mai creduto. Penso che questa sia stata una delle vittorie più belle, se non la più bella, della mia carriera a livello di club".

Questo il tabellino di Milan-Ferencvaros, match valido per la quarta giornata della fase campionato di Europa League

MILAN (4-4-1-1): Torriani; Terracciano, De Winter, Diawara (dal 46’ Gila), Bartesaghi (dal 67’ Camarda); Hutchinson, Jashari (dal 64’ Modric), Musah (dal 46’ Moreira), Cisse (dal 46’ Pulisic); Rabiot; Ramos. A disp.: Bouyer, Pittarella; Estupiñan, Vladimirov; Comotto. All.: Amorim.

FERENCVÁROS (4-3-3): Dibusz; Cadu, Raemaekers, Gómez, Osváth; Corbu (dal 90’+3 Arzani), Á. Nagy, Bero (dal 67’ Madarász); Birmančević (dal 86’ Levi), Joseph (dal 67’ Yusuf), Zachariassen (dal 86’ Maïga). A disp.: Á.Varga, Bagi, Keïta, O. Nagy, Rommens, Z.Varga, Zohoré. All.: Borbély.

Ammoniti: 32’ Cisse, 51’ De Winter, 90’+4 Nagy.

#2423. Serafini: "Quando avevo letto 'giornate di riposo fino a martedì da sabato' ero rimasto un po' perplesso"

Luca Serafini, giornalista, si è così espresso su MilanCommunityTV commentando la sconfitta del Milan contro il Ferencvaros: "È evidente che di quella vestigia europea, di quella tradizione che ha accompagnato il Milan persino negli anni di Berlusconi, che quasi si concentrava più nelle manifestazioni internazionali che in campionato, non è rimasto nulla. Questa Europa League al Milan evidentemente non interessa. Siamo tornati in campo completamente svuotati dal derby: quando avevo letto «giornata di riposo fino a martedì, da sabato», ero rimasto un po' perplesso; forse c'era bisogno di ricaricare le batterie, invece sono svuotate completamente".

Serafini, poco dopo, ha precisato le responsabilità di questa brutta sconfitta: "Poi non è una questione di turnover: il Milan non riesce a mettere in fila due partite positive. E a parte il turnover, c'è un problema di mentalità grosso, perché i giocatori che vanno in campo dovrebbero metterci l'anima, dovrebbero dimostrare di essere da Milan, dovrebbero dimostrare di essere all'altezza. Invece, dopo un primo tempo scialbo, nel secondo tempo, a parte i primi 5 minuti con una bella occasione per pareggiare, poi la squadra si è sfaldata completamente.

Un pasticcio enorme tra De Winter e Torriani, che erano stati fra i due più decorosi del primo tempo: pasticcio tremendo ha favorito il gol del Ferencváros, che poi ha raddoppiato in un contropiede da calcio d'angolo, stile a Torino quando fece gol Morata con la gestione Pioli, con tutti nella metà campo del Ferencváros e il Ferencvaros parte in contropiede e raddoppia".

#2418. Inter, un dato è identico al Como prima dello scontro diretto del Meazza

Inter, un dato è identico al Como prima dello scontro diretto del Meazza

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Cristian Chivu vuole solidità dalla sua Inter. Ma senza portarla per questo motivo a snaturarsi rispetto a quanto fatto in stagione. L'identità della squadra nerazzurra è chiara ed emerge anche da un dato perfettamente uguale al Como che domenica arriva a San Siro. Lo rivela la Gazzetta dello Sport di oggi:

"Il primo esame per dare continuità alla bontà di una rinnovata solidità difensiva sarà domenica contro il professor Fabregas. Non il test più semplice al momento, contro una squadra lanciatissima e galvanizzata dai 9 gol segnati in Champions alla vittima Lens. Il Como è quarto in classifica, ma è anche il sesto attacco della Serie A con 18 reti, 8 in meno dell’Inter.

Un dato avvicina le due lombarde a due giorni dal primo confronto stagionale: entrambe hanno effettuato 81 recuperi offensivi in campionato; tuttavia il Como ha realizzato 4 gol in seguito a queste situazioni di gioco, più di qualsiasi altra squadra e il doppio rispetto ai due dell’Inter. Antenne dritte ad Appiano: i comaschi sono un cliente complicato e un Fabregas che non ha mai gioito contro i nerazzurri, così come un Douvikas mai a segno contro i milanesi di Chivu sono ulteriori motivi di slancio".

#2417. Raimondi su Cisse: "Contro il Ferencvaros ha floppato. La maglia del Milan è pesante per tutti, anche per chi ha talento come lui"

Claudio Raimondi, intervenuto a SportMediaset su Italia Uno, ha commentato così le prestazioni negative di Ardon Jashari e Alphadjo Cisse contro il Ferencvaros in Europa League: "Il Milan sta aspettando da tanto Jashari. È arrivato due estati fa, pagato non poco, cirrac 37 milioni di euro. È uno di quei giocatori che chiede più spazio, ma poi si capisce perché non viene schierato perché le opportunità che gli vengono date vengono sprecate. Contro il Ferencvaros si è visto un giocatore molto lento, impacciato, fiacco, con poca personalità, ha commesso troppi errori e quindi la grande domanda è: ma davvero è un giocatore da Milan? Non è il caso di fare qualche riflessione, magari valutare se è il caso di cederlo in prestito?

Tra i bocciati anche Cisse. Lo abbiamo spesso elogiato, celebrato, il suo talento non si discute, ma forse contro il Frencvaros è mancata la voglia, la fame di questo ragazzo che ha stupito anche proprio in campo internazionale con quei tre gol a Salisburgo. Arrivava da 10 minuti giocati nel derby, quindi era fresco, riposato e carico. E' arrivata anche la convocazione in Nazionale, ma contro il Ferencvaros ha floppato. Quella del Milan è una maglia pesante per tutti, anche per chi ha talento come lui".

#2416. Valentin Carboni, dopo l'accordo Inter-Racing arriva la decisione inattesa

Valentin Carboni, dopo l'accordo Inter-Racing arriva la decisione inattesa

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Sembrava ormai a un passo dal ritorno in campo, ma Valentin Carboni dovrà aspettare ancora. Il talento argentino, di proprietà dell'Inter e attualmente in prestito al Racing Avellaneda, non ha ancora ottenuto il via libera dei medici dopo il grave infortunio al ginocchio subito lo scorso febbraio.

Nelle ultime settimane il classe 2005 aveva intensificato gli allenamenti, tornando anche a svolgere alcune esercitazioni insieme ai compagni. Segnali incoraggianti che avevano alimentato la speranza di rivederlo tra i convocati già per la partita contro il Barracas Central.

Alla fine, però, è arrivata una decisione diversa.

Secondo quanto riferisce Racing de Alma, il corpo medico e lo staff tecnico guidato da Chirola Romero hanno deciso di non accelerare i tempi. Carboni non è ancora considerato pronto per disputare una partita ufficiale e resterà quindi fuori dalla lista dei convocati.

Il giocatore sta completando la fase conclusiva della riabilitazione dopo la rottura del legamento crociato anteriore del ginocchio destro, rimediata durante un allenamento lo scorso febbraio.

La sua evoluzione viene giudicata positivamente, ma i medici non intendono concedere il via libera prima di avere garanzie sufficienti sulle condizioni fisiche del calciatore.

L'obiettivo è evitare una possibile ricaduta dopo un infortunio particolarmente delicato.

Non si tratta dunque di un nuovo problema fisico, ma di una scelta prudenziale che potrebbe allungare ulteriormente il periodo di inattività del giovane argentino.

I nerazzurri hanno già raggiunto un accordo con il Racing per prolungare il prestito di Carboni fino al 31 dicembre 2027, consentendo al giocatore di restare in Argentina per un'altra stagione.

Una decisione maturata proprio alla luce del lungo stop che ha compromesso il suo 2026.

Carboni, arrivato al Racing a gennaio, ha infatti disputato appena cinque partite ufficiali prima di essere fermato dall'infortunio.

Adesso la priorità è recuperare completamente e ritrovare la migliore condizione atletica. La possibilità di rivederlo in campo entro la fine dell'anno si è progressivamente ridotta e, secondo le ultime indiscrezioni argentine, il rientro potrebbe essere rimandato alla preparazione per la stagione 2027.

Nessuna data definitiva è stata ancora fissata, ma al Racing prevale una linea molto chiara: meglio aspettare qualche settimana in più che rischiare di compromettere nuovamente il recupero del giocatore.

#2415. Rambaudi avvisa la Lazio: “Non devi andare a Napoli e pensare di gestire la partita”

Le analisi e i consigli di Roberto Rambaudi in vista del big match di Serie A fra Lazio e Napoli

In vista della sfida contro il Napoli, l'ex giocatore della Lazio Roberto Rambaudi ha analizzato a Radiosei la pesante assenza di Mattia Zaccagni e l'approccio tattico e mentale con cui la squadra capitolina dovrà affrontare il match al Maradona.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Zaccagni è quello che salta l'uomo, fa assist, crea superiorità; sarà un test per chi prende il suo posto. Ad ogni modo, nonostante il Napoli sia in difficoltà, la vittoria non è mai scontata. Sono forti ma non sono nella condizione mentale ottimale. Immagino la squadra verrà contestata; alcuni giocatori, i più importanti e rappresentativi, proveranno a dare una scossa, in casa, prima della sosta. Non devi andare a Napoli e pensare di gestire la partita, credo che molto si deciderà nei primi 20-25', se li fai galvanizzare o se li metti subito in difficoltà, creando dall'inizio.

Se Tavares e Pedraza stanno bene, potrebbe essere un'opzione vedere loro due dal 1' a sinistra. Credo sia una scelta che Gattuso farà in base a come vede i calciatori durante la settimana. Magari anche Cancellieri a sinistra può essere una soluzione, io Isaksen da destra non lo toglierei.

#2414. Prima di ieri, Torriani non aveva mai giocato da titolare con la maglia del Milan

Prima presenza da titolare per Lorenzo Torriani con la maglia del Milan tra tutte le competizioni (quarta gara per lui con i rossoneri in assoluto). Lo riporta Opta.

Ruben Amorim, allenatore del Milan, ha parlato in conferenza stampa al termine di Milan-Ferencvaros, sfida valida per la quarta giornata della Europa League Phase.

"Non si tratta della formazione titolare, si tratta della preparazione, di essere pronti mentalmente a vincere ogni partita e della responsabilità che abbiamo come club. Non importa chi scende in campo dal primo minuto, dobbiamo lottare per ogni seconda palla, curare i piccoli dettagli. Non parlo di tecnica, di fare magie o di vincere ogni singola gara: parlo delle piccole cose, di ciò che possiamo controllare. E oggi, specialmente nel primo tempo, non ci sono spiegazioni. Non ho nulla per difendere me stesso e i miei giocatori per la prestazione di oggi. La gestione dell'undici iniziale è legata anche al fatto che, se riproponiamo certi calciatori troppo spesso, rischiamo gli infortuni; devo gestire i minuti, devo immaginare come si svilupperà la partita, ma non voglio parlare della formazione titolare. Voglio dire che oggi non ci sono scuse per la nostra prestazione, soprattutto nei primi 45 minuti. Naturalmente lotteremo in questa competizione fino alla fine, ma la verità è che sembra che non riusciamo a mantenere lo stesso livello a distanza di tre giorni, ed è su questo che dobbiamo lavorare."

#2410. Ravezzani: "Il gioco dominante di cui parlava Amorim è quello che dovrebbe salvarti proprio quando non ci sono i protagonisti"

Fabio Ravezzani, giornalista, si è così espresso sul suo canale Youtube commentando la sconfitta del Milan contro il Ferencvaros: "Adesso Amorim si arrampica sugli specchi e dice 'Mah, forse è un problema di carattere, forse anche l'anno scorso c'erano questi tipi di problemi, forse non riusciamo a uscire da un certo tipo di competizione, il derby, e entrare mentalmente in un'altra', ma sono spiegazioni tutto sommato che non lasciano nessun tipo di possibilità di capire cosa stia succedendo. È un parlare a vuoto per cercare di spiegare qualcosa che non c'è".

Ravezzani cerca di analizzare così i problemi del Milan di Amorim: "Allora cerchiamo di capire cosa non c'è nel Milan di adesso e io mi permetto di dirlo con una certa chiarezza e nettezza: non c'è il gioco. Cioè, questa è una squadra a cui non abbiamo mai visto fare delle partite brillantissime con un gioco che si imponeva su quello degli avversari. Lo ripeto, anche nel derby il Milan non ha dominato l'Inter, ha usato le giuste contromarce. L'Inter è partita come sempre in cavalleria tutta sparata in avanti e poi il Milan è riuscito a contenere, a colpire in maniera implacabile, ha fatto una bella partita da questo punto di vista, forse anche tatticamente intelligente, ma non dominante.

E allora il gioco che dovrebbe dare Amorim, il profeta di calcio nuovo moderno, è quello che dovrebbe salvarti proprio quando non ci sono i protagonisti, proprio quando tu non hai contro una squadra molto forte che in qualche modo ti piega perché nessuno viene in campo per farsi dominare, tanto più se è una squadra forte e importante come è stato nel derby l'Inter o come sono state le altre grandi squadre che ha affrontato il Milan; perché dobbiamo dire anche questo: il Milan per esempio ha subito tantissimo la Lazio, ha subito enormemente la Juventus, poi è riuscito a pareggiare queste partite, però sono state partite in cui il Milan ha sofferto le pene dell'inferno.

Quando si è trattato di dire il Milan adesso che mette sotto l'avversario l'abbiamo visto poche volte e Ferencvaros era proprio questo tipo di squadra, cioè una squadra in cui anche se c'erano delle riserve al posto dei titolari, doveva esserci un gioco tale per cui si vedeva il Milan dominare e il Ferencvaros magari giocare in contropiede, giocare una partita coperta, prudente. È stato l'esatto opposto".

#2409. Panchina Napoli, idea Conceição: la sfida con la Lazio è un dentro o fuori per Allegri

In attesa del dentro o fuori contro la Lazio, la panchina azzurra valuta il profilo di Sergio Conceição come possibile erede.

La pesante sconfitta subita contro il Porto in Champions League e le difficoltà iniziali della stagione hanno fatto scattare i primi dubbi sulla permanenza di Massimiliano Allegri, con indiscrezioni provenienti direttamente dalla Francia precisamente da Foot Mercato, che accostano con insistenza la panchina del Napoli a Sergio Conceição. Questa voce di mercato indica inoltre che la prossima sfida di campionato contro la Lazio rappresenterà un vero e proprio dentro o fuori per il futuro di Allegri.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Il profilo del tecnico lusitano non rappresenta una novità per la dirigenza partenopea, che aveva già tentato di ingaggiarlo nel 2021 e nel 2023. In entrambe le occasioni, però, la trattativa era sfumata a causa dell'alta richiesta economica del Porto per la clausola rescissoria. Oggi lo scenario è completamente differente, dato che l'allenatore ha guidato il Milan nella seconda parte della stagione 2024/25 ed è attualmente svincolato, pronto a firmare subito per una nuova squadra.

Forte del recente rifiuto alla proposta avanzata dal Fenerbahçe, l'allenatore attende una chiamata da uno dei cinque principali campionati europei, aprendo concretamente le porte a un ritorno in Serie A. Per il momento si tratta unicamente di una candidatura e non di una trattativa in corso, con il futuro di questa pista che dipenderà strettamente dalle prossime prove della squadra e dal test quasi decisivo di domenica contro la Lazio.

#2407. Premio Bulgarelli Number 8 2025-2026: il Premio Legends a Demetrio Albertini

(ANSA) - BOLOGNA, 06 NOV - Lunedì 9 novembre nella Cappella Farnese di Palazzo d'Accursio a Bologna, sarà consegnato il premio 'Bulgarelli Number 8', intitolato allo storico numero 8 del Bologna e tra i fondatori dell'Aic-Associazione italiana calciatori che lo organizza. Ad essere premiati per la stagione 2025-2026 sono Kenan Yildiz della Juventus come miglior calciatore e la compagna di squadra Martina Rosucci come miglior calciatrice. Tra gli allenatori vincono Cesc Fàbregas del Como per la Serie A maschile e Gianluca Grassadonia della Lazio per il calcio femminile. Miglior arbitro è Daniele Doveri, della sezione di Roma.

Il 'Premio Legendsì va a Demetrio Albertini, ex centrocampista di Milan e Nazionale. Alla cerimonia saranno presenti Fabio Capello, presidente del premio, Umberto Calcagno, presidente dell'Aic, Maurizio Fabbri, presidente dell'Assemblea legislativa dell'Emilia-Romagna, e l'assessora allo Sport del Comune di Bologna Roberta Li Calzi. L'iniziativa è sostenuta dalla Regione e ha il patrocinio del Comune di Bologna. (ANSA).

#2406. Marazzina: "Scudetto? Inter può inciampare. Non vince i derby perché..."

Marazzina: "Scudetto? Inter può inciampare. Non vince i derby perché..."

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Massimo Marazzina, ex attaccante tra le altre anche dell'Inter, ai microfoni di News.Superscommesse.it ha parlato dei nerazzurri a due giorni dalla gara di campionato con il Como. Qui le sue considerazioni:

"Non considero l'Inter nettamente favorita, ma comunque la principale candidata allo scudetto. Può sempre inciampare, come abbiamo visto nel derby, dove ha meritato di perdere. Allo stesso tempo, non vedo altre squadre in grado di impensierirla più di tanto. La Lazio difficilmente riuscirà a mantenere questo ritmo. Al di là del fatto che stia facendo molto bene e giochi un ottimo calcio, credo sia quasi impossibile continuare così fino alla fine. È vero che non ha le coppe, ma non la considero una squadra strutturata per sostenere un simile rendimento per tutto il campionato. Mi auguro che nelle ultime due o tre giornate ci siano almeno un paio di squadre a contendersi il titolo, altrimenti il finale rischia di diventare monotono. Un po' di adrenalina sarebbe l'ideale".

L'Inter non vince un derby da due anni e mezzo: come spieghi questa difficoltà?

"È una situazione piuttosto strana. Non credo che il Milan sia una vera e propria bestia nera dell'Inter, né che ci sia una particolare situazione tattica o psicologica. Il derby è certamente una partita particolare, ma i giocatori nerazzurri sono abituati a gestire appuntamenti importanti, come quelli in Champions League. Non credo sia nemmeno un merito esclusivo del Milan, perché anche i rossoneri cambiano ogni anno. Secondo me è una coincidenza legata alla particolarità del derby, una serie di episodi sfavorevoli. Semplicemente, può capitare una giornata storta. E all'Inter, ultimamente, capita proprio contro il Milan".

Dopo le ultime vittorie in Champions, dove può arrivare questa Inter in Europa? Può competere con le grandi?

"L'Inter ha vinto due partite consecutive, ma non è diventata improvvisamente una squadra straordinaria, così come non era diventata scarsa quando aveva perso. Bisogna mantenere equilibrio nei giudizi. Può giocarsela con le grandi squadre? Certamente, lo ha dimostrato anche negli anni passati. Lasciando da parte le finali perse, il fatto stesso di arrivarci dimostra il valore della squadra. In Champions League ogni partita è una storia a sé, soprattutto in una competizione che prevede sfide secche. Bisogna valutare il momento in cui si affronta una determinata squadra, le condizioni dei giocatori e gli eventuali infortuni. Quando si arriverà alle partite di andata e ritorno, il discorso cambierà ulteriormente. In una gara secca, invece, può capitare di affrontare una squadra sulla carta più forte, ma in un momento di difficoltà o con qualche assenza importante. Allo stesso modo, una squadra inferiore può incontrare l'Inter in condizioni favorevoli".

#2405. Beukema in mixed zone: “Non possiamo prendere questi gol. Siamo arrabbiati”

“Certo, vogliamo dimostrare questa risposta a tutti, ma anche a noi stessi, perché sappiamo le nostre qualità, abbiamo qualità dentro questo gruppo. Però, se non vinci e hai di nuovo un’altra delusione oggi, non è ovviamente facile. #2404. Gomes scores for Aston Villa in defeat by Bayern Munich in Hong Kong

Aston Villa’s tour of the Far East ended in defeat as they lost 2-1 to German champions Bayern Munich in a pre-season friendly at the Kai Tak Sports Park in Hong Kong on Friday.

Bayern took the lead inside the final 10 minutes of the first half, with defender Kim Min-jae heading in from Tom Bischof’s sublime free-kick. Villa goalkeeper Marco Bizot made a string of saves in the second half to keep Bayern’s lead at one goal until former Liverpool forward Luis Díaz smashed in Bayern’s second off the underside of the bar.

Just as the game appeared to be out of reach for Aston Villa, who had struggled to create much going forward for most of the game, Unai Emery’s side showed some life late on. Summer signing João Gomes’s goal from the edge of the box gave them hope before Tammy Abraham nearly brought them back onto level terms, only to be denied by a fine save from 40-year-old Manuel Neuer.

Aston Villa are next in action in six days’ time when they take on Paris St-Germain in the Uefa Super Cup before kicking off their Premier League campaign at Brighton. Bayern have one more friendly to come against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.

Stepping up in the big moments! 🐐🧤 pic.twitter.com/r14rF39XpR

Unai Emery’s men head home from the Far East to face PSG in the Uefa Super Cup in Salzburg next week and then have one more pre-season friendly against Borussia Monchengladbach before starting their Premier League campaign at Brighton.

Possession: Aston Villa 44%-56% Bayern MunichShots: 8-16Shots on target: 3-8Corners: 3-7

Bizot nearly has a calamitous moment coming out of his penalty area but gets away with it.

The final whistle is blown soon after and Bayern hold on to beat Villa 2-1 in Hong Kong. They had looked comfortable when Diaz put them 2-0 up but Villa kept going until the end.

Victory in the final match of the #AudiFCBTour! 💪🔥 pic.twitter.com/UFhQhme62i

The space opens up for Palhinha to shoot from range but Bizot makes a fairly comfortable save down to his right.

It was all Bayern for 80 minutes of this game but Villa have come to life in the closing stages, with Vincent Kompany’s side now holding on a bit.

When Diaz scored Bayern’s second goal, Aston Villa were out of this game but it has been all Villa since then. They look the more likely to get a goal.

Gomes’ free-kick out on the left is parried behind by Neuer for a Villa corner on the right. The corner comes to nothing.

For 80 minutes, Aston Villa barely looked like scoring but come so close to getting two goals in just a few minutes. Abraham meets a cross from the right and must think he has scored but his header is brilliantly saved by the 40-year-old Neuer.

Maybe this game is not quite done and dusted yet. A cross from the right picks out Gomes on the edge of the box. The Brazilian takes one touch before toe poking past Neuer and into the Bayern net. Gomes meant that finish and Villa have some hope.

Abraham should have done better there. He has the space to drive down Villa’s right and has a decision to make; cross the ball to his left and drive forward himself to shoot. He takes a while to make the decision before his attempted cross is intercepted by Ito.

Boey’s yellow card was his final act of the game as he is replaced by Della Rovere. Boey did seem to be struggling with some cramp.

Bayern receive their second yellow card of the game as Boey is booked for cynically pulling down Lynch.

Cash, Maatsen and Buendia have gone off for Aston Villa.

Double substitution from Bayern as Pavic and Assomo replace Tah and Brown.

A fantastic goal to put the Germans 2-0 up and it is nothing more than they deserve. Ibrahimovic finds Diaz in space on the left and the Colombian drives at Cash. As he enters the Villa box, Diaz cuts inside onto his preferred right foot and smashes a shot towards goal. It appeared to go right over Bizot, who opts not to put a hand up. The ball crashes off the underside of the bar and goes in. It has been all Bayern in this second half and that goal had been coming.

Bayern are knocking on the door and come very close to doubling their lead. Diaz’s shot from the edge of the box looks destined to hit the net with Bizot beaten but fortunately for Villa Abraham is on hand on his own goal line to make the block.

Bizot makes another great save. Bayern win a free-kick out on the right after Brown was fouled. Bischoff opts to shoot instead of crossing and nearly beats Bizot at his near post but the Aston Villa keeper gets across to his left to make the save.

Unai Emery makes a double substitution as Mings and Lynch replace Torres and Guessand. Mings takes the captain’s armband.

That is a fantastic double save from Bizot, first denying Stanisic and then Ibrahimovic. Both of those shots came through bodies so Bizot did a good job of reacting and making the saves with his legs on both occasions.

It is safe to say the Bayern fans out in Hong Kong love Diaz, who is cheered every time he touches the ball.

Vincent Kompany is going to make a triple change as Ito, Diaz and Palhinha come on. Former Liverpool man Diaz received a great ovation from the fans in Hong Kong. Palhinha, who spent last season on loan at Tottenha, has been linked with a move to Aston Villa in this window.

Should Bayern have been awarded a penalty there? A ball is played in behind the Villa defence into the penalty area and Ibrahimovic appeared to be brought down but the referee waves those appeals away. On first viewing, that appeared to be a penalty.

Just before the hour mark, it is time for some changes from Aston Villa. Abraham and Bogarde come on for Madjo and Kamara. This is Abraham’s first appearance of this pre-season.

Good play from Gomes earns Aston Villa a free-kick not far outside the Bayern penalty area out on the left, in a similar position to the free-kick that Bayern scored from in the first half. Buendia clips in a good ball to the back post, where Guessand looks likely to score but instead misses the ball on the stretch. Should Guessand have done better with that? Probably.

Brown was starting in his customary left-back position for Bayern in the first half but is now operating out on the right wing.

Gomes leaves one late on Pavlovic and, had this been a competitive fixture, I suspect that might have been a yellow card.

Tah is brought down by Gomes and Bayern will have a free-kick out on the right not a million miles outside the Villa penalty area. The delivery though is poor and easily cleared away.

Bayern have brought Stanisic on at half-time for Laimer. Aston Villa have not made a change at the break.

Possession: Aston Villa 45%-55% Bayern MunichShots: 4-7Shots on target: 1-2Corners: 1-2

The first half in Hong Kong is brought to an end and Bayern take a 1-0 lead into the break courtesy of Kim’s goal.

There will be one added minute at the end of this first half.

Aston Villa have struggled to create much going forward and have not really had a genuine goal-scoring opportunity in this first half. Their forward line though will look quite different when they kick off the seaosn than it does today.

The German side take the lead in Hong Kong. Bischof’s left-footed delivery from a free-kick out on the left is sublime, whipping it right into the corridor of uncertainty. The quality of delivery means it is quite a simple header for Kim, who makes no mistake with his header into the far corner. Kim might have been offside but obviously there is no VAR in use today. 1-0 Bayern.

That is a waste in the end from Bayern. Boey fires a pass in-field into Laimer, who takes a fantastic first touch on the turn inside the Villa half. He drives through the Villa half and at the penalty area but his pass outside looking for Cardozo is poor and overhit, with the ball rolling out for a goal kick. Laimer will be annoyed at himself having done all the hard work and then failed to execute the easy part.

Bischof eyes one up from range and tries to pick out the top corner. Bizot is routed to his spot and is hoping the shot goes wide. Bischof’s effort is always curling wide, much to Bizot’s relief.

The first booking of the game goes the way of Bayern Munich. Guessand drives forward into the Bayern half and is brought down by a cynical foul from Cardozo. You were half expecting Cardozo to perhaps escape without a booking with it being a pre-season friendly but he does not. It was the right call to book him.

That was lovely from Pavlovic. The German midfielder shows good feet to beat Gomes before getting his shot away from a central position just outside the box. He forces Bizot into a decent save but one he should be making.

Bayern force Villa into an error and win themselves a corner as a result out on the left. It is delivered into a dangerous area at the front post but Villa do a good job of handling it and clearing their lines.

Bayern Munich have made two signings this summer; Brown from Bundesliga rivals Frankfurt and Saibari from PSV. Brown is starting at left-back for Bayern today with Saibari not involved as he closes in on a return from a knock picked up at the World Cup with Morocco. Brown feels like such a Bayern signing, nabbing a good player from a Bundesliga rival.

Aston Villa have made four signings so far this summer but just one is in their starting XI today in the form of Gomes, who joined from Midlands rivals Wolves this summer. Young Swiss midfielder Manzambi has joined from Freiburg but is currently out injured. Garnacho picked up a knock and Cisse, who has arrived from LASK, is not involved for Villa today.

The ball is played in behind the Aston Villa defence to pick out the run of Binder. On the edge of the Villa box, Binder gets the first touch but Lindelof is calm in defence and heads back to his keeper Bizot.

Moments later, Gomes is dispossessed inside his own half and Ibrahimovic has a shot from just outside the box but drags it wide of the far post.

Good work from 17-year-old Madjo down Aston Villa’s left wins them their first corner of the game. Buendia’s inswinger is headed away too easily at the near post. Aston Villa have seen their former set-piece coach Austin MacPhee nabbed this summer by Xabi Alonso’s Chelsea.

The referee is allowing the game to flow and thus not blowing for soft fouls.

The first chance of the game falls to Bayern Munich. Brown’s cross from the left is headed by Maatsen straight to Ibrahimovic on the edge of the box. Ibrahimovic, not any relation to Zlatan, takes the shot on first time but it is blocked by Maatsen, who makes up for that poor header with the block. The ball goes behind for a corner, which comes to nothing.

Interestingly, there are only eight outfield substitutions on the Aston Villa bench for this game, with the likes of Garnacho and McGinn picking up knocks the other day. It appears then that at least a couple of Villa outfield players will have to play the full 90.

Both sides emerge from the tunnel, Aston Villa led out by Matty Cash and Bayern Munich by Manuel Neuer, and we are closing in on kick-off in Hong Kong. A group of delegates shake the hands of both starting XIs.

Chelsea and Juventus played in this stadium the other night and it is safe to say that the pitch does not look in the best state ahead of kick-off today. It looks like it will continue to rip up and the last thing the two managers will want is to pick up any serious injuries.

Currently, before the two sides emerge, the crowd in Hong Kong are being treated to some pre-match dancing and entertainment, which includes people dressed as pandas and sharks dancing. Would the Premier League ever take inspiration and have dancing pandas before kick-off?

Aston Villa: Bizot, Cash, Lindelof, Torres, Maatsen, Kamara, Joao Gomes, Hemmings, Buendia, Guessand, Madjo.Substitutes: Wright, Asemota, Mings, Abraham, Nedejkovic, Bogarde, Rowe, Burrowes, Carroll, Lynch.

Bayern Munich: Neuer, Kim, Tah, Brown, Boey, Laimer, Bischof, Pavlovic, Ibrahimovic, Binder, Cardozo. Substitutes: Ulrich, Podar-Stiube, Urbig, Kimmich, Luis Díaz, Palhinha, Ito, Chávez, Pavic, Bara, Manuba, Stanisic, Della Rovere, Assomo.

The likes of Ollie Watkins, Ezri Konsa and Emi Martinez are yet to return to training after their World Cup exploits and Aston Villa are also without captain John McGinn and new signing Alejandro Garnacho, who picked up knocks in their win over BG Pathum the other day.

Aston Villa have already sold Youri Tielemans and Morgan Rogers this summer, with Lucas Digne on the verge of a move to PSG, but could another starter be on the way out of Villa Park? England defender Ezri Konsa has been linked with moves to the likes of Arsenal and Liverpool but will Unai Emery be able to keep hold of the 28-year-old?

It has been a big summer of change at Aston Villa already and there could be more changes before the end of the window. Left-back Lucas Digne is set to move to PSG and is set to be replaced by Atlético Madrid’s Matteo Ruggeri. Joao Palhinha, who is on the bench for Bayern Munich today, has been linked with a move to Aston Villa.

“Aston Villa are a top side with a very good coach. They have a large squad and will field plenty of talent. They have already played more friendlies than us, but for us it is a good challenge. It is a match in which we will learn a lot about our team’s fitness. It will be a good match for us.”

“It is difficult to improve; after all, it is not just about the trophies we have won, but also about the number of goals and matches we have won. It is possible, but difficult. Right now, I would say our aim is to win the Champions League; there is no doubt about that. And throughout the season, I will be saying that we need to focus on the next match. To achieve that; to win the Bundesliga, the cup and the Champions League, we need the whole squad to be fit and healthy. We need to make good use of them and improve the squad. We have already done that, but hopefully it will help us achieve our goals.”

July 21- Walsall 0 Aston Villa 5July 25- Porto 2 Aston Villa 1July 28- Aston Villa 2 Real Sociedad 4August 1- Indonesia All Stars 1 Aston Villa 3August 4- BG Pathum 1 Aston Villa 3Today- Aston Villa vs Bayern MunichAugust 12- PSG vs Aston Villa (Uefa Super Cup)August 15- Borussia Monchengladbach vs Aston Villa

Aston Villa’s preparations for the new season continue out in Hong Kong as they take on Bundesliga champions Bayern Munich at the Kai Tak Stadium. These sides have met twice before; Aston Villa beat Bayern 1-0 to win the European Cup in 1981/82 before then meeting in the Champions League group stages during the 2024/25 season, with Jhon Duran’s long-range screamer giving Aston Villa a 1-0 win at Villa Park.

After losing two of their first three pre-season friendlies, Aston Villa have won their first two games on Asian soil and this game marks the end of their tour of the Far East. It has been a summer of significant change at Aston Villa thus far with some key members of their squad departing and some new faces arriving. Youri Tielemans has departed after Manchester United triggered his release clause and they then sold Morgan Rogers to Chelsea for a British transfer record £117m. Unai Emery’s side have brought in 20-year-old Swiss midfielder Johan Manzambi, Brazilian midfielder Joao Gomes and Alejandro Garnacho, with the deal for Garnacho being an initial loan. Manzambi is currently on the sidelines due to a knee injury.

Unai Emery’s side were dealt a blow during the World Cup as Belgian midfielder Amadou Onana picked up a serious knee injury. They were though boosted by the return of Boubacar Kamara after seven months out as he scored in the 3-1 friendly victory over BG Parthum. As one midfielder returned from injury in that game, another picked up a fresh injury as John McGinn suffered a knock to his knee. The likes of Ollie Watkins, Ezri Konsa and Emi Martinez are yet to return after their world Cup exploits. They begin their Premier League campaign with an away game on the south coast against Brighton on August 23 but will be involved in the Uefa Super Cup against PSG next week having lifted the Europa League last season.

Bayern Munich head into this season looking to win a third straight Bundesliga title and have won two of their three friendlies, losing once. They have added Moroccan attacker Ismael Saibari from PSV and German left-back Nathaniel Brown from Bundesliga rivals Eintracht Frankfurt to their ranks this summer. The likes of Harry Kane, Dayot Upamecano and Michael Olise are not available as they are still yet to return after World Cup duty but Vincent Kompany has confirmed that Bayern’s German stars are set to return.

After today, Bayern have one more friendly against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.

#2397. Malagò: “Galliani? Non mi sentirete mai spendere parole non positive su di lui”

Le dichiarazioni del commissario straordinario tra la corsa alla Federcalcio e i retroscena sulle grandi scelte del passato

Giovanni Malagò, commissario e candidato alla presidenza FIGC, traccia il bilancio delle sfide del calcio italiano in alcune dichiarazioni riportate dall'ANSA, spaziando dalla sua candidatura e il rapporto con Galliani fino alle scelte sul commissariamento e le Olimpiadi.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Ho portato la candidatura perché mi sembrava doveroso dopo essere stato eletto il 22 giugno con una maggioranza importante e le tante vicende che sono successe tra pantouflage, tesseramento e dopo aver difeso l'autonomia del calcio. Galliani? Non posso che rispettarlo e stimarlo, quello che ci differenzia non è un tema anagrafico, non mi sentirete mai spendere parole non positive su di lui. Sicuramente è molto focalizzato sul mondo del calcio, io cerco di portare innovazioni e coraggio che vengono anche da altri mondi all'interno del calcio che penso di conoscere bene.

Se fossimo andati al voto in quella Giunta ne saremmo usciti tutti sconfitti. Quella è stata una soluzione che non so dirvi se la migliore, ma sicuramente la meno peggio. Non mi brucia, ma è come se avessi una ferita che si è cicatrizzata. Fu una scelta scellerata che portò a dei disastri a chi l'ha presa in termini politico-elettorali, però dopo sei mesi ci ritrovammo a occuparci di Milano Cortina. Latina fu fortemente penalizzata da quella decisione. Avremmo vinto e avremmo coinvolto questa zona come abbiamo fatto con tanti territori per Milano.
